📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期麻疹病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 通过咽拭子中的实时逆转录聚合酶链反应检测 麻疹病毒 RNA。患者出现发烧和头尾斑丘疹,随后出现进行性全身和呼吸道症状,包括咳嗽、咽炎和轻微呼吸疼痛。通过支持治疗,氧合作用迅速改善,并且没有并发症或继发病例记录。
  • 麻疹 血清流行率 在1-5岁、6-15岁、16-40岁和>40岁年龄组中分别从2013年的33%、41%、47%和48%增加到2022年的77%、71%、89%和96%。 [10.1080/21645515.2026.2750748]。
  • 在横断面分析中,cART 6-12 个月后,麻疹 血清阳性率按开始年龄划分没有统计学差异(0-8 个月:30.8% [n=12/39];9-23 个月:37.0% [n=51/138];24-59 个月:26.6% [n=33/124])。
  • 宾夕法尼亚州 1,400 多例 麻疹 确诊病例中有 4 例是接种疫苗的个体,阿勒格尼县 93.8% 的儿童已完全接种 麻疹 疫苗,一些社区低于群体免疫所需的 95% 阈值。 [10.64628/aai.urq9hvnxa]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Measles virus RNA was detected by real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction in a pharyngeal swab.
  • Measles seroprevalence increased from 33%, 41%, 47%, and 48% in the 1-5, 6-15, 16-40, and >40 years age groups in 2013 to 77%, 71%, 89%, and 96% respectively in 2022. [10.1080/21645515.2026.2750748].
  • In the cross-sectional analysis, measles seropositivity did not statistically differ after 6-12 months of cART by age at initiation (0-8: 30.8% [n=12/39]; 9-23: 37.0% [n=51/138]; 24-59 months: 26.6% [n=33/124]).
  • Four of the over 1,400 confirmed measles cases in Pennsylvania were among vaccinated individuals, and 93.8% of children in Allegheny County are fully vaccinated against measles, with some neighborhoods below the 95% threshold needed for herd immunity.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Journal of acquired immune deficiency syndromes (1999)  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-03  |  DOI: 10.1097/qai.0000000000003999  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

赞比亚农村地区艾滋病毒感染儿童中抗逆转录病毒药物 麻疹病毒 的启动时间和 麻疹病毒 血清阳性。

Timing of antiretroviral treatment initiation and seropositivity to measles virus among children living with HIV in rural Zambia.
作者： Catherine Gayle Sutcliffe, Saki Takahashi, Amanda G. Finney, ScM, Mutinta Hamahuwa, Nkumbula Moyo, Amy K. Winter, Hellen K. Matakala, Mathias Muleka, Dip. Biomed
摘要中文翻译背景 联合抗逆转录病毒治疗 (cART) 降低了 HIV 感染儿童 (CHIV) 的发病率,但不能恢复 疫苗 诱导的 麻疹 免疫力。许多 CHIV 现在在 麻疹 麻疹 麻疹 血清流行率 之前启动 cART,但对 麻疹 血清流行率 的影响尚不清楚。本研究比较了赞比亚 CHIV 启动 cART 9 个月前后的 麻疹 血清阳性率。方法 这项回顾性研究嵌套在 队列研究 CHIV 研究和 2007 年至 2020 年在赞比亚马查进行的基于社区的研究中。通过酶免疫分析对 0-8、9-23 和 24-59 个月龄开始 cART 的 CHIV 样本以及基于社区的年龄匹配对照样本进行 麻疹 IgG 抗体测试。横断面分析比较了经过 6-12 个月的 cART 和对照组后 CHIV 组之间的 麻疹 血清流行率。纵向分析评估了从 9 个月大到 48 个月 cART 期间以及 2010-11 麻疹 暴发 期间的抗体浓度。结果 在横断面分析中,cART 6-12 个月后,麻疹 血清阳性率按开始年龄划分没有统计学差异(0-8 个月:30.8% [n=12/39];9-23 个月:37.0% [n=51/138];24-59 个月:26.6% [n=33/124])。在 CHIV 组中,血清阳性率显着低于年龄匹配的对照组(63.5%、75.7% 和 80.3%)。在 242 名 CHIV 患者中进行纵向随访,其中 46-56% 出现抗体反应,估计血清逆转仅需 1.8-3.2 年。2010-11 年 CHIV 感染者中,50% 出现血清转化或增强,估计在 2.1 年内出现血清转化。结论 麻疹 在 CHIV 中的 麻疹 水平较低,即使在 9 个月龄前开始 cART 时也是如此,而且血清逆转的时间很短。这些发现支持这样的建议:CHIV(包括婴儿)将受益于免疫重建后定期重新接种疫苗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 联合抗逆转录病毒治疗 (cART) 降低了 HIV 感染儿童 (CHIV) 的发病率,但不能恢复 疫苗 诱导的 麻疹 免疫力。
设计与对象
本研究比较了赞比亚 CHIV 启动 cART 9 个月前后的 麻疹 血清阳性率。
核心方法
通过酶免疫分析对 0-8、9-23 和 24-59 个月龄开始 cART 的 CHIV 样本以及基于社区的年龄匹配对照样本进行 麻疹 IgG 抗体测试。
主要结果
在横断面分析中,cART 6-12 个月后,麻疹 血清阳性率按开始年龄划分没有统计学差异(0-8 个月:30.8% [n=12/39];9-23 个月:37.0% [n=51/138];24-59 个月:26.6% [n=33/124])。
解释与创新
即使在 9 个月大之前开始 cART,麻疹 在 CHIV 中的 血清流行率 也较低,而且血清回复的时间很短。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持这样的建议:CHIV(包括婴儿)将受益于免疫重建后定期重新接种疫苗。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体限制。

Timing of antiretroviral treatment initiation and seropositivity to measles virus among children living with HIV in rural Zambia.

Authors: Catherine Gayle Sutcliffe, Saki Takahashi, Amanda G. Finney, ScM, Mutinta Hamahuwa, Nkumbula Moyo, Amy K. Winter, Hellen K. Matakala, Mathias Muleka, Dip. Biomed
Original abstract (English)
Background Combination antiretroviral therapy (cART) reduces morbidity among children living with HIV (CHIV) but does not restore vaccine-induced measles immunity. Many CHIV now initiate cART before measles vaccination but the impact on measles seroprevalence is not understood. This study compared measles seropositivty between CHIV initiating cART before and after 9 months in Zambia. Methods This retrospective study was nested within a cohort study of CHIV and community-based studies in Macha, Zambia conducted from 2007-2020. Samples were tested for measles IgG antibodies by enzyme immunoassay from CHIV initiating cART at ages 0-8, 9-23, and 24-59 months and age-matched community-based controls. A cross-sectional analysis compared measles seroprevalence between CHIV groups after 6-12 months of cART and controls. A longitudinal analysis evaluated antibody concentrations from age 9 months through 48 months of cART and during the 2010-11 measles outbreak. Results In the cross-sectional analysis, measles seropositivity did not statistically differ after 6-12 months of cART by age at initiation (0-8: 30.8% [n=12/39]; 9-23: 37.0% [n=51/138]; 24-59 months: 26.6% [n=33/124]). Within CHIV groups, seropositivity was significantly lower than age-matched controls (63.5%, 75.7%, and 80.3%). Among 242 CHIV followed longitudinally, 46-56% had an antibody response, with estimated seroreversion in only 1.8-3.2 years. Among CHIV in 2010-11, 50% seroconverted or boosted with estimated seroreversion in 2.1 years. Conclusions Measles seroprevalence was low among CHIV, even when starting cART before 9 months of age, and the time to seroreversion was short. These findings support recommendations that CHIV, including infants, would benefit from periodic revaccination after immune reconstitution.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Background Combination antiretroviral therapy (cART) reduces morbidity among children living with HIV (CHIV) but does not restore vaccine-induced measles immunity.
Design and population
This study compared measles seropositivity between CHIV initiating cART before and after 9 months in Zambia.
Core methods
Samples were tested for measles IgG antibodies by enzyme immunoassay from CHIV initiating cART at ages 0-8, 9-23, and 24-59 months and age-matched community-based controls.
Main results
In the cross-sectional analysis, measles seropositivity did not statistically differ after 6-12 months of cART by age at initiation (0-8: 30.8% [n=12/39]; 9-23: 37.0% [n=51/138]; 24-59 months: 26.6% [n=33/124]).
Interpretation and novelty
Measles seroprevalence was low among CHIV, even when starting cART before 9 months of age, and the time to seroreversion was short.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support recommendations that CHIV, including infants, would benefit from periodic revaccination after immune reconstitution.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations to the study design.

Journal: Pediatrics  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-01  |  DOI: 10.1542/peds.2025-075651  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 158, 5, e2025075651
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

疫苗 诱导 ALL 儿童对 麻疹 和水痘的抗体和 T 细胞免疫。

Vaccine-Induced Antibody and T Cell Immunity to Measles and Varicella in Children With ALL.
作者： Janna R Shapiro, Alina D Dorogy, Michelle Science, Sumit Gupta, Sarah Alexander, Shelly Bolotin, Tania H Watts, Shapiro JR, Dorogy AD, Science M
摘要中文翻译摘要 患有急性淋巴细胞白血病(ALL)的儿童接受多药化疗,这会导致免疫系统发生深刻的变化。关于疾病和治疗如何影响抗原特异性免疫记忆的数据有限,导致该人群重新接种疫苗的最佳实践指南不一致。在此,为了向 疫苗 提供指导,我们研究了在儿童 ALL 治疗期间和治疗后是否维持了源自常规儿童 麻疹 和水痘带状疱疹病毒 (VZV) 疫苗 的免疫力。我们报告说,与健康对照 (n = 13) 相比,ALL 儿童 (n = 45) 的 麻疹 和 VZV 抗体显着减少,特别是在距离最近一次 疫苗 剂量已经过去较长时间的年龄较大的儿童中。然而,麻疹 和 VZV 特异性抗体的亲合力在各组之间无法区分。尽管 T 细胞区室的组成发生了变化,但 ALL 儿童的整体 T 细胞功能和抗原特异性 T 细胞功能均得以保留。这些数据为接受 ALL 治疗 治疗的儿童重新接种疫苗提供了令人信服的证据。完整的 T 细胞反应表明 治疗 后的重新接种是有效的。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查儿童急性淋巴细胞白血病(ALL)治疗期间和治疗后是否能维持常规儿童麻疹和水痘带状疱疹病毒(VZV)疫苗产生的免疫力,解决有关疾病和治疗如何影响抗原特异性免疫记忆的有限数据,导致该人群重新接种疫苗的最佳实践指南不一致。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 45 名 ALL 儿童和 13 名健康对照者,调查疾病和治疗对 麻疹 和 VZV 特异性 免疫应答 的影响。
核心方法
该研究测量了 ALL 儿童和健康对照儿童的抗体水平和 T 细胞功能,比较结果以评估 ALL 治疗 对免疫记忆的影响。
主要结果
与健康对照相比,ALL 儿童的 麻疹 和 VZV 抗体水平显着降低,尤其是自最近一次 疫苗 剂量以来间隔时间较长的年龄较大的儿童。然而,麻疹 和 VZV 特异性抗体的亲合力在各组之间无法区分。
解释与创新
这些数据为儿童在 ALL 治疗 后重新接种疫苗提供了令人信服的证据,完整的 T 细胞反应表明 治疗 后重新接种疫苗是有效的。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对 疫苗 指导具有影响,表明重新接种疫苗对于维持 ALL 儿童的免疫力是必要的,并且 治疗 后重新接种疫苗将是有效的。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性包括样本量小和关注特定人群,这可能会限制普遍性。然而,根据观察到的 免疫应答,有强有力的证据表明需要重新接种疫苗。

Vaccine-Induced Antibody and T Cell Immunity to Measles and Varicella in Children With ALL.

Authors: Janna R Shapiro, Alina D Dorogy, Michelle Science, Sumit Gupta, Sarah Alexander, Shelly Bolotin, Tania H Watts, Shapiro JR, Dorogy AD, Science M
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Children with acute lymphoblastic leukemia (ALL) are treated with multiagent chemotherapy that causes profound changes to the immune system. There are limited data on how disease and therapy impact antigen-specific immune memory, leading to inconsistent guidelines on best practices for revaccination of this population. Here, to inform vaccine guidance, we investigated whether immunity derived from routine childhood measles and varicella zoster virus (VZV) vaccines is maintained during and after therapy for childhood ALL. We report that antibodies against measles and VZV were significantly reduced in children with ALL (n=45) compared to healthy controls (n=13), particularly in older children in whom a longer time had passed since their most recent vaccine dose. However, the avidity of the measles and VZV-specific antibodies was indistinguishable between groups. Despite changes to the composition of the T cell compartment, both overall and antigen-specific T cell function were preserved in children with ALL. These data provide compelling evidence for revaccination of children following ALL treatment. Intact T cell responses suggest that post-treatment revaccination would be effective.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether immunity derived from routine childhood measles and varicella zoster virus (VZV) vaccines is maintained during and after therapy for childhood acute lymphoblastic leukemia (ALL), addressing the limited data on how disease and therapy impact antigen-specific immune memory, leading to inconsistent guidelines on best practices for revaccination of this population.
Design and population
The study involved 45 children with ALL and 13 healthy controls, investigating the impact of disease and therapy on measles and VZV-specific immune responses.
Core methods
The study measured antibody levels and T cell function in children with ALL and healthy controls, comparing the results to assess the impact of ALL treatment on immune memory.
Main results
Antibody levels against measles and VZV were significantly reduced in children with ALL compared to healthy controls, particularly in older children with longer intervals since their most recent vaccine dose. However, the avidity of the measles and VZV-specific antibodies was indistinguishable between groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The data provide compelling evidence for revaccination of children following ALL treatment, with intact T cell responses suggesting that post-treatment revaccination would be effective.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings have implications for vaccine guidance, suggesting that revaccination is necessary to maintain immunity in children with ALL, and that post-treatment revaccination would be effective.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations include the small sample size and the focus on a specific population, which may limit generalizability. However, the evidence is strong for the need for revaccination based on the observed immune responses.

Journal: Human vaccines & immunotherapeutics  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-04  |  DOI: 10.1080/21645515.2026.2750748  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 22, 1, 2750748
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

印度南部韦洛尔市 MR 运动前后 5 年的人群对 麻疹 和风疹的免疫力。

Population level immunity to measles and rubella 5 years before and after the MR campaign in urban Vellore, South India.
作者： Ramya Madhavan, Akilandeswari Eswaran, Vaishnavi Gandhi, Lakshmi Raj, Poornima Saravanan, Reshma Raju, Julian Vivek Leander Xavier, Jovin Stanley Joseph, Prasanna Samuel Premkumar, Kavitha Selvaraj
摘要中文翻译印度的目标是到 2026 年消除 麻疹 和风疹 (MR)。尽管 NFHS-5 和 NFHS-6 报告的 麻疹 覆盖率超过 90%,但评估人群水平的免疫差距对于指导保护弱势群体的策略仍然至关重要。我们利用了 2013 年和 2022 年在韦洛尔市进行的两次基于社区的血清调查的生物样本库样本,代表了全国 MR 疫苗接种 运动前后 5 年的时期。使用 ELISA 对年龄和性别分层的参与者进行 麻疹 和风疹特异性 IgG 抗体检测。对各项调查中的 血清流行率 估计值进行比较,并使用广义加性模型 (GAM) 来评估特定年龄的血清阳性模式。使用混合建模方法估计 麻疹 和风疹的有效繁殖数 (Re)。总共分析了 2013 年的 1,029 个样本和 2022 年的 718 个样本。麻疹 血清流行率 在 1-5、6-15、16-40 和 >40 岁年龄组中分别从 2013 年的 33%、41%、47% 和 48% 增加到 2022 年的 77%、71%、89% 和 96%。风疹 血清流行率 从同一年龄组中这一比例为 32%、67%、76% 和 73% 至 93%、84%、93% 和 98%。两种感染均未观察到显着的性别差异。MR 活动期后,麻疹 的估计 Re 从 7.4 下降至 3.6,风疹的估计 Re 从 1.8 下降至 0.8。到 2022 年,人口免疫力得到改善,尤其是风疹免疫力。然而,1-15 岁儿童中较低的 麻疹 血清流行率 表明,尽管 疫苗 覆盖率较高,但仍存在持续的免疫缺口,这凸显了印度需要持续的 麻疹 和有针对性的策略来实现 麻疹 风疹消除。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
对各项调查中的 血清流行率 估计值进行比较,并使用广义加性模型 (GAM) 来评估特定年龄的血清阳性模式。
设计与对象
我们利用了 2013 年和 2022 年在韦洛尔市进行的两次基于社区的血清调查的生物样本库样本,代表了全国 MR 疫苗接种 运动前后 5 年的时期。
核心方法
总共分析了 2013 年的 1,029 个样本和 2022 年的 718 个样本。
主要结果
麻疹 血清流行率 在1-5岁、6-15岁、16-40岁和>40岁年龄组中分别从2013年的33%、41%、47%和48%增加到2022年的77%、71%、89%和96%。
解释与创新
在同一年龄组中,风疹 血清流行率 从 32%、67%、76% 和 73% 增加到 93%、84%、93% 和 98%。
科研与公卫意义
然而,1-15 岁儿童中较低的 麻疹 血清流行率 表明,尽管 疫苗 覆盖率较高,但仍存在持续的免疫缺口,这凸显了印度需要持续的 麻疹 和有针对性的策略来实现 麻疹 风疹消除。
局限与证据强度
MR 活动期后,麻疹 的估计 Re 从 7.4 下降至 3.6,风疹的估计 Re 从 1.8 下降至 0.8。

Population level immunity to measles and rubella 5 years before and after the MR campaign in urban Vellore, South India.

Authors: Ramya Madhavan, Akilandeswari Eswaran, Vaishnavi Gandhi, Lakshmi Raj, Poornima Saravanan, Reshma Raju, Julian Vivek Leander Xavier, Jovin Stanley Joseph, Prasanna Samuel Premkumar, Kavitha Selvaraj
Original abstract (English)
India aims to eliminate measles and rubella (MR) by 2026. Despite >90% vaccination coverage reported in NFHS-5 and NFHS-6, assessing population-level immunity gaps remains essential to guide strategies for protecting vulnerable groups. We leveraged biobanked samples from two community-based serosurveys conducted in urban Vellore in 2013 and 2022, representing periods 5 years before and after the nationwide MR vaccination campaign. Age- and sex-stratified participants were tested for measles- and rubella-specific IgG antibodies using ELISA. Seroprevalence estimates were compared across surveys, and generalized additive models (GAM) were used to assess age-specific seropositivity patterns. Effective reproduction numbers (Re) for measles and rubella were estimated using mixture modeling approaches. A total of 1,029 samples from 2013 and 718 samples from 2022 were analyzed. Measles seroprevalence increased from 33%, 41%, 47%, and 48% in the 1-5, 6-15, 16-40, and >40 years age groups in 2013 to 77%, 71%, 89%, and 96% respectively in 2022. Rubella seroprevalence increased from 32%, 67%, 76%, and 73% to 93%, 84%, 93%, and 98% across the same age groups. No significant sex-based differences were observed for either infection. The estimated Re declined from 7.4 to 3.6 for measles and from 1.8 to 0.8 for rubella following the MR campaign period. Population immunity improved by 2022, particularly for rubella. However, lower measles seroprevalence among children aged 1-15 years indicates persistent immunity gaps despite high vaccine coverage, highlighting the need for continued surveillance and targeted strategies to achieve measles-rubella elimination in India.
View research seven-element analysis

Design and population
We leveraged biobanked samples from two community-based serosurveys conducted in urban Vellore in 2013 and 2022, representing periods 5 years before and after the nationwide MR vaccination campaign.
Core methods
A total of 1,029 samples from 2013 and 718 samples from 2022 were analyzed.
Main results
Measles seroprevalence increased from 33%, 41%, 47%, and 48% in the 1-5, 6-15, 16-40, and >40 years age groups in 2013 to 77%, 71%, 89%, and 96% respectively in 2022.
Interpretation and novelty
Rubella seroprevalence increased from 32%, 67%, 76%, and 73% to 93%, 84%, 93%, and 98% across the same age groups.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

麻疹 使用基于电子病历的系统进行注册筛查。

Measles Screening at Registration Using an Electronic Medical Record-Based System.
作者： Marc A Mazade
摘要中文翻译位于 2025 年 麻疹 区域 麻疹 附近的儿科医疗保健系统共享在注册时开发基于电子病历的 麻疹 筛查的流程,以及时识别 麻疹 高风险患者并防止二次暴露。通过报告登记时间和微调筛查,以迭代地将筛查阳性率降低至每 1000 次接触 0.12 例,获得了担心登记速度缓慢和空中隔离室数量有限的利益相关者的支持。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种基于电子病历的 麻疹 登记筛查系统,以迅速识别高危患者并防止二次 麻疹 暴露,解决对登记速度慢和隔离室有限的担忧。
设计与对象
位于 2025 麻疹 暴发 附近的儿科医疗保健系统实施了一个筛查系统,以在登记时识别 麻疹,不断将筛查阳性率降低至每 1000 次就诊 0.12 例。
核心方法
该系统的开发是通过报告注册时间和微调筛选过程来获得利益相关者的支持。
主要结果
筛查系统成功实施,将筛查阳性率降低至每 1000 次遇到 0.12 例。
解释与创新
该研究证明了使用电子病历进行挂号时麻疹筛查的可行性,减轻了医疗资源的负担。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为在其他医疗机构中实施类似的筛查系统提供参考,以改善 麻疹 麻疹 监测 和预防。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括缺乏随机对照组以及利益相关者的认可可能存在偏差,这可能会限制研究结果的普遍性。

Measles Screening at Registration Using an Electronic Medical Record-Based System.

Authors: Marc A Mazade
Original abstract (English)
A pediatric healthcare system located near a 2025 measles regional outbreak shares processes for developing electronic medical records-based measles screening at registration to promptly identify patients at high risk of measles and prevent secondary exposures. Buy-in was gained from stakeholders concerned about slowed registration and limited numbers of airborne isolation rooms by reporting registration times and fine-tuning screening to reduce screening positivity iteratively to rates as low as 0.12 per 1000 encounters.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop an electronic medical record-based measles screening system at registration to promptly identify high-risk patients and prevent secondary measles exposures, addressing concerns about slowed registration and limited isolation rooms.
Design and population
A pediatric healthcare system, located near a 2025 measles outbreak, implemented a screening system to identify measles at registration, iteratively reducing screening positivity to 0.12 per 1000 encounters.
Core methods
The system was developed by gaining stakeholder buy-in through reporting registration times and fine-tuning the screening process.
Main results
The screening system was successfully implemented, reducing screening positivity to 0.12 per 1000 encounters.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated the feasibility of using electronic medical records for measles screening at registration, reducing the burden on healthcare resources.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could inform the implementation of similar screening systems in other healthcare settings to improve measles surveillance and prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the lack of a randomized control group and the potential for bias in stakeholder buy-in, which may limit the generalizability of the findings.

加拿大27年后麻疹淘汰失利:2025-2026年五大省麻疹浪潮的数学模型分析

Canada’s loss of measles elimination after 27 years: a mathematical modelling analysis of the five major provincial outbreak waves in 2025–2026
作者： Chapin S. Korosec, Lily McChesney-Knight, Edward Wolfgang Thommes, Monica Gabriela Cojocaru, Korosec, C. S., Thommes, E. W., Cojocaru, M. G.
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 加拿大在 2025 年丧失 麻疹 消除状态凸显了免疫缺口集中的人群的脆弱性。仅省级 发病率 无法轻易地区分少数易感人群中相对强烈的 传播 和涉及较大人群的 传播。我们量化了 2025-2026 年五个省 麻疹 波中的这些差异。方法 我们将常见的易感者-暴露-感染-恢复 (SEIR) 模型与 2025 年安大略省以及 2025 年和 2026 年阿尔伯塔省和马尼托巴省每周报告的 麻疹 发病率 进行拟合。平均潜伏期和感染期固定为 10 天和 8 天。我们估计了有效传染数 ( R eff,0 )、有效易感人群 ( S eff,0 ),并分析了初始感染人数,以评估省份和特定波次的主要病例模型估计。我们计算初始指数增长率、倍增时间和半峰全宽 (FWHM),以表征早期流行病增长以及每波峰值周围 发病率 升高的持续时间。研究结果 2025 年,安大略省的有效繁殖数 R eff,0 为 1.9,模型估计的有效易感人群 S eff,0 为 3,101 人,初始倍增时间为 2.4 周,峰值半高宽为 12.3 周。艾伯塔省的 R eff 为 0,为 1.9,S eff 为 0,为 2,601 人,倍增时间为 2.4 周,FWHM 为 12.1 周。马尼托巴省的 R eff 为 1.5,S eff 为 0,为 1.5,S eff 为 0,为 490 人,倍增时间较长,为 4.0 周,半高宽为 20.5 周。2026年,艾伯塔省的R eff,0 增加至2.1,而S eff,0 减少至383人;倍增时间缩短至 2.1 周,半高宽缩短至 9.7 周。马尼托巴省的 R eff,0 增加至 1.8,S eff,0 增加至 1,038 个体,而倍增时间缩短至 2.6 周,FWHM 缩短至 13.0 周。阿尔伯塔省较小的第二波将最高的有效繁殖数与最短的倍增时间和峰值持续时间结合在一起,表明有限的 暴发 大小可以与快速的 传播 共存。对 传播 强度、流行速度、高峰持续时间和有效易感人群的联合估计为比较 传播 和为公共卫生规划提供信息提供了补充措施。总之,这些估计为区分 传播 强度与易感性参与规模提供了定量基础,支持 暴发 之间的比较,并告知未来 传播 公共卫生应对的时间和规模。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过量化 2025-2026 年五个省 麻疹 浪潮中 麻疹 强度和规模的差异,了解加拿大 麻疹 消除状况的丧失。强调了免疫缺口集中的人群的脆弱性。
设计与对象
该研究使用常见的易感者-暴露-感染-恢复 (SEIR) 模型来拟合 2025 年安大略省以及 2025 年和 2026 年阿尔伯塔省和马尼托巴省每周报告的 麻疹 发病率 数据。
核心方法
SEIR 模型适合每周报告的 麻疹 发病率 数据,固定平均潜伏期和感染期分别为 10 天和 8 天。估计有效繁殖数 (R_eff,0)、有效易感人群 (S_eff,0) 和初始感染数,以评估省份和特定波次的原发病例模型估计。
主要结果
安大略省的 R_eff,0 为 1.9,有 3,101 名有效易感个体,倍增时间为 2.4 周,峰值 FWHM 为 12.3 周。阿尔伯塔省和曼尼托巴省的 R_eff,0 值相似,分别为 1.9 和 1.5,但有效易感人群和倍增时间各不相同。2026 年,阿尔伯塔省的 R_eff,0 增加到 2.1,而曼尼托巴省则增加到 1.8。
解释与创新
阿尔伯塔省较小的第二波具有最高的有效繁殖数、最短的倍增时间和峰值持续时间,这表明有限的 暴发 大小可以与快速的 传播 共存。对 传播 强度、流行速度、高峰持续时间和有效易感人群的联合估计为比较 传播 和为公共卫生规划提供信息提供了补充措施。
科研与公卫意义
这些估计为区分 传播 强度与易感性参与规模提供了定量基础,支持 传播 之间的比较,并告知未来 传播 公共卫生应对的时间和规模。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计局限性包括在不同省份使用通用的 SEIR 模型以及固定的平均潜伏期和传染期。证据强度中等,因为模型估计基于报告的 发病率 数据,并且没有考虑所有潜在的混杂因素。

Canada’s loss of measles elimination after 27 years: a mathematical modelling analysis of the five major provincial outbreak waves in 2025–2026

Authors: Chapin S. Korosec, Lily McChesney-Knight, Edward Wolfgang Thommes, Monica Gabriela Cojocaru, Korosec, C. S., Thommes, E. W., Cojocaru, M. G.
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Canada’s loss of measles elimination status in 2025 highlighted the vulnerability of populations with concentrated immunity gaps. Provincial incidence alone cannot readily distinguish relatively intense transmission within a small susceptible population from transmission involving a larger population. We quantified these differences across five provincial measles waves in 2025-2026. Methods We fitted a common susceptible–exposed–infectious–recovered (SEIR) model to weekly reported measles incidence for Ontario in 2025 and Alberta and Manitoba in 2025 and 2026. Mean latent and infectious periods were fixed at 10 and 8 days. We estimated the effective reproduction number ( R eff , 0 ), effective susceptible population ( S eff , 0 ), and profiled the initial infected number to evaluate province and wave-specific primary case model estimates. We calculate the initial exponential growth rate, doubling time, and peak full width at half maximum (FWHM) to characterize early epidemic growth and the duration of elevated incidence around each wave’s peak. Findings In 2025, Ontario’s effective reproduction number, R eff , 0 , was 1.9, with a model-estimated effective susceptible population, S eff , 0 , of 3,101 individuals, an initial doubling time of 2.4 weeks, and a peak FWHM of 12.3 weeks. Alberta had a similar R eff , 0 of 1.9, with S eff , 0 of 2,601 individuals, a doubling time of 2.4 weeks, and FWHM of 12.1 weeks. Manitoba had a lower R eff , 0 of 1.5 and S eff , 0 of 490 individuals, with a longer doubling time of 4.0 weeks and FWHM of 20.5 weeks. In 2026, Alberta’s R eff , 0 increased to 2.1 while S eff , 0 decreased to 383 individuals; doubling time shortened to 2.1 weeks and FWHM to 9.7 weeks. Manitoba’s R eff , 0 increased to 1.8 and S eff , 0 to 1,038 individuals, while doubling time shortened to 2.6 weeks and FWHM to 13.0 weeks. Interpretation Alberta’s smaller second wave combined the highest effective reproduction number with the shortest doubling time and peak duration, demonstrating that limited outbreak size can coexist with rapid transmission. Joint estimates of transmission intensity, epidemic pace, peak duration, and effective susceptible population provide complementary measures for comparing outbreaks and informing public-health planning. Together, these estimates provide a quantitative basis for distinguishing transmission intensity from the scale of susceptible involvement, supporting comparisons across outbreaks and informing the timing and scale of public-health responses for future outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the loss of measles elimination status in Canada by quantifying the differences in transmission intensity and scale across five provincial measles waves in 2025-2026. The vulnerability of populations with concentrated immunity gaps was highlighted.
Design and population
The study used a common susceptible–exposed–infectious–recovered (SEIR) model to fit weekly reported measles incidence data from Ontario in 2025 and from Alberta and Manitoba in 2025 and 2026.
Core methods
The SEIR model was fitted to weekly reported measles incidence data, with fixed mean latent and infectious periods of 10 and 8 days, respectively. The effective reproduction number (R_eff,0), effective susceptible population (S_eff,0), and initial infected number were estimated to evaluate province and wave-specific primary case model estimates.
Main results
Ontario had an R_eff,0 of 1.9 with 3,101 effective susceptible individuals, a doubling time of 2.4 weeks, and a peak FWHM of 12.3 weeks. Alberta and Manitoba had similar R_eff,0 values of 1.9 and 1.5, respectively, with varying effective susceptible populations and doubling times. In 2026, Alberta’s R_eff,0 increased to 2.1, while Manitoba’s increased to 1.8.
Interpretation and novelty
Alberta’s smaller second wave had the highest effective reproduction number with the shortest doubling time and peak duration, demonstrating that limited outbreak size can coexist with rapid transmission. Joint estimates of transmission intensity, epidemic pace, peak duration, and effective susceptible population provide complementary measures for comparing outbreaks and informing public-health planning.
Scientific and public-health significance
These estimates provide a quantitative basis for distinguishing transmission intensity from the scale of susceptible involvement, supporting comparisons across outbreaks and informing the timing and scale of public-health responses for future outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s design limitations include the use of a common SEIR model across different provinces and the fixed mean latent and infectious periods. The evidence strength is moderate, as the model estimates are based on reported incidence data and do not account for all potential confounding factors.

西得克萨斯州麻疹 暴发带给我的公共卫生领导力启示

What the West Texas Measles Outbreak Taught Me About Public Health Leadership.
作者： Katherine Wells, Wells K., Katherine M Wells
摘要中文翻译

What the West Texas Measles Outbreak Taught Me About Public Health Leadership.

Authors: Katherine Wells, Wells K., Katherine M Wells
Original abstract (English)
Commentary What the West Texas Measles Outbreak Taught Me About Public Health Leadership - Katherine Wells Journal of Public Health Management & Practice 32(6):p 852-853, November/December 2026. | DOI: 10.1097/PHH.0000000000002404 Copyright © 2026 Wolters Kluwer Health, LLC. All rights reserved.

The study aimed to understand the lessons learned from the West Texas measles outbreak to inform public health leadership. The author discusses the public health challenges and leadership strategies needed during such outbreaks.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

具有不完整 疫苗接种 和 1 个免疫力减弱的 麻疹 的 SVIR 模型分析:Sidoarjo-Indonesia 案例

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case
作者： Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
摘要中文翻译麻疹仍然是印度尼西亚的重大公共卫生挑战,尤其是在疫苗接种覆盖率异质性和免疫力下降的地区。本研究开发并分析了一个针对麻疹传播的易感-接种-感染-康复(SVIR)分组模型,该模型纳入了免疫力下降的因素。基本再生数R0是通过下一代矩阵方法推导的,并且通过李雅普诺夫函数严格证明了无病平衡态和地方性平衡态的全局稳定性。模型参数是从2023年Sidoarjo市每周的监测数据中使用粒子群优化(PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)进行评估。PSO方法实现了最低的MAPE(11.17%),优于其他方法。估计的R0 = 0.668确认了无病状态。归一化敏感性分析确定疫苗效力是最关键的参数;疫苗效力下降至0 .75会使R0接近临界阈值,任何进一步的减少都会使系统进入地方性状态。这些发现强调了通过加强剂接种计划和强化疫苗接种策略保持高疫苗效力的必要性,以在区级维持麻疹的消除。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
使用下一代矩阵方法推导出基本繁殖数R0,并通过Lyapunov函数严格证明了无病平衡和地方病平衡的全局稳定性。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
本研究开发并分析了印度尼西亚诗都阿佐市麻疹 传播 的易感-疫苗接种-感染-康复 (SVIR) 区室模型,其中考虑了免疫力的减弱。
主要结果
模型参数是根据西多阿佐市 2023 年每周的 监测 数据使用粒子群优化 (PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波器、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差 (MAPE) 进行评估。PSO 实现了最低的 MAPE (11.17%),优于其他方法。
解释与创新
估计的 R0 = 0.668 确认无病状态。
科研与公卫意义
麻疹 在印度尼西亚仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战,特别是在 麻疹 覆盖范围不均匀且免疫力下降的地区。
局限与证据强度
Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case

Authors: Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a significant public health challenge in Indonesia, especially in regionswith heterogeneous vaccination coverage and waning immunity. This study develops andanalyzes a Susceptible–Vaccinated–Infected–Recovered (SVIR) compartmental model formeasles transmission in Sidoarjo City, Indonesia, incorporating waning immunity. The basicreproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and the globalstability of both the disease-free and endemic equilibria is rigorously proven via Lyapunovfunctions. Model parameters are estimated from weekly 2023 surveillance data in Sidoarjo Cityusing Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), Extended Kalman Filter, Maximum LikelihoodEstimation, and Least Squares, and evaluated via Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE).PSO achieves the lowest MAPE (11.17%), outperforming the other methods. The estimatedR0 = 0.668confirms a disease-free regime. Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.

2025 年乌干达 Kamwenge 区难民收容环境中的长期 麻疹 暴发

Protracted measles outbreak in a refugee-hosting setting in Kamwenge District, Uganda, 2025
作者： Nasif Matovu, Richard Migisha, Kyomugisha Denise Aman, Deborah Aujo, Yasiini Nuwamanya, Benon Kwesiga, Lilian Bulage, Brenda Nakafeero Ssimbwa, Fred Nsubuga, Immaculate Ampeire
摘要中文翻译尽管进行了麻疹-风疹(MR)疫苗接种,乌干达仍持续发生麻疹暴发。2025年6月24日,作为难民营所在地的卡姆温盖区报告了三例实验室确诊的麻疹病例。我们调查了此次麻疹暴发,以确定其规模、识别风险因素,并估计麻疹疫苗的有效性(VE)和接种覆盖率(VC)。疑似病例定义为2025年2月1日至7月7日期间,居住在卡姆温盖区的居民出现发热和全身性斑丘疹,并伴有咳嗽、流涕或结膜炎中的至少一种症状;确诊病例为麻疹特异性IgM阳性。病例通过医疗机构记录和社区调查确定。我们计算了攻击率(AR),进行了环境评估,并采用邻近社区匹配的病例对照研究。条件逻辑回归分析用于估计调整后的比值比(aOR)。疫苗有效性(VE)计算为(1−OR_M−H)×100;接种覆盖率(VC)为符合条件的对照中接种疫苗的比例。我们共识别出109例病例(3例确诊病例,106例疑似病例),未发生死亡。其中48.6%的病例为难民。总体攻击率为3.2/10,000,儿童(9–17个月)和难民的攻击率最高,分别为30.8/10,000和13.3/10,000。在73例病例和167例对照中,参加宗教场所活动与感染相关(aOR = 11.1;95% CI 3.5–35.1),而MR1(aOR = 0.3;95% CI 0.1–0.7)和MR2(aOR = 0.05;95% CI 0.01–0.3)具有保护作用。MR1的有效性为93%,MR2的有效性为95%。符合条件的对照中MR1的接种覆盖率为67%,MR2为36%。在麻疹疫苗接种覆盖率低且社会接触频繁的情况下,发生了持续的麻疹暴发。加强常规两剂麻疹疫苗接种、在难民营社区开展有针对性的疫苗接种以及对疑似病例进行及时隔离,可能有助于预防未来的麻疹暴发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管 麻疹-风疹 (MR) 疫苗接种,麻疹 暴发 继续在乌干达发生。该研究旨在调查 暴发 以确定其大小、识别风险因素并估计 疫苗 有效性 (VE) 和 疫苗接种 覆盖范围 (VC)。
设计与对象
该研究于 2025 年 2 月 1 日至 7 月 7 日在难民收容区 Kamwenge 区进行。研究涉及 73 例病例和 167 例对照,病例是通过卫生设施记录和社区搜索发现的。
核心方法
条件逻辑回归估计调整优势比 (aOR)。该研究计算了发病率(AR),进行了环境评估,并使用邻里对照进行了年龄匹配的病例对照研究。
主要结果
我们发现了 109 例病例(3 例确诊,106 例疑似);没有发生死亡事件。在病例和对照中,参加礼拜场所与感染相关(aOR = 11.1;95% CI 3.5-35.1),而 MR1(aOR = 0.3;95% CI 0.1-0.7)和 MR2(aOR = 0.05;95% CI 0.01-0.3)具有保护性。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了社会混合和低 疫苗接种 覆盖率在维持 麻疹 暴发 中的作用。该研究为 MR 疫苗 在难民收容社区中的有效性提供了新颖的见解。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,在难民收容社区加强常规两剂 疫苗接种 和针对性 疫苗接种 可能有助于预防未来的 暴发。迅速隔离疑似病例也被强调为一项重要的公共卫生措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据因其对卫生设施记录和社区搜索的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入回忆偏差。由于可能存在漏报和样本量较小,因此调查结果的可信度为中等。

Protracted measles outbreak in a refugee-hosting setting in Kamwenge District, Uganda, 2025

Authors: Nasif Matovu, Richard Migisha, Kyomugisha Denise Aman, Deborah Aujo, Yasiini Nuwamanya, Benon Kwesiga, Lilian Bulage, Brenda Nakafeero Ssimbwa, Fred Nsubuga, Immaculate Ampeire
Original abstract (English)
Measles outbreaks continue to occur in Uganda despite measles-rubella (MR) vaccination. On 24 June 2025, Kamwenge District, a refugee-hosting area, reported three laboratory-confirmed measles cases. We investigated the outbreak to determine its magnitude, identify risk factors, and estimate vaccine effectiveness (VE), and vaccination coverage (VC). A suspected case was fever and generalized maculopapular rash with ≥ 1 of cough, coryza, or conjunctivitis among Kamwenge residents from 1 February to 7 July 2025; a confirmed case had measles-specific IgM. Cases were identified through health-facility records and community searches. We calculated attack rates (AR), conducted environmental assessments, and performed an age-matched case-control study using neighborhood controls. Conditional logistic regression estimated adjusted odds ratios (aORs). VE was calculated as (1−OR_ M−H )×100; VC was the proportion of vaccinated eligible controls. We identified 109 cases (3 confirmed, 106 suspected); no deaths occurred. Of the cases, 48.6% were refugees. Overall AR was 3.2/10,000, highest among children aged 9–17 months (30.8/10,000) and refugees (13.3/10,000). Among 73 cases and 167 controls, attending a place of worship was associated with infection (aOR = 11.1; 95% CI 3.5–35.1), while MR1 (aOR = 0.3; 95% CI 0.1–0.7) and MR2 (aOR = 0.05; 95% CI 0.01–0.3) were protective. VE was 93% for MR1 and 95% for MR2. Coverage among eligible controls was 67% for MR1 and 36% for MR2. A prolonged outbreak occurred amid low MR coverage and high-contact social mixing. Strengthening routine two-dose vaccination, targeted vaccination in refugee-hosting communities, and prompt isolation of suspected cases may help prevent future outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles outbreaks continue to occur in Uganda despite measles-rubella (MR) vaccination. The study aimed to investigate the outbreak to determine its magnitude, identify risk factors, and estimate vaccine effectiveness (VE), and vaccination coverage (VC).
Design and population
The study was conducted in Kamwenge District, a refugee-hosting area, from 1 February to 7 July 2025. It involved 73 cases and 167 controls, with cases identified through health-facility records and community searches.
Core methods
Conditional logistic regression estimated adjusted odds ratios (aORs). The study calculated attack rates (AR), conducted environmental assessments, and performed an age-matched case-control study using neighborhood controls.
Main results
We identified 109 cases (3 confirmed, 106 suspected); no deaths occurred. Among the cases and controls, attending a place of worship was associated with infection (aOR = 11.1; 95% CI 3.5–35.1), while MR1 (aOR = 0.3; 95% CI 0.1–0.7) and MR2 (aOR = 0.05; 95% CI 0.01–0.3) were protective.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the role of social mixing and low vaccination coverage in sustaining measles outbreaks. The study provides novel insights into the effectiveness of MR vaccines in refugee-hosting communities.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that strengthening routine two-dose vaccination and targeted vaccination in refugee-hosting communities could help prevent future outbreaks. Prompt isolation of suspected cases is also emphasized as a critical public health measure.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by its reliance on health-facility records and community searches, which may introduce recall bias. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the potential for underreporting and the small sample size.

巴基斯坦旁遮普邦的零剂量儿童和 麻疹 风险:程度、地理分布和规划影响,2022-2026 年

Zero-dose children and measles risk in Punjab, Pakistan: magnitude, geographic distribution, and programmatic implications, 2022–2026
作者： Muhammad Mehroz Saleem, Mufakker Mian, Marvi Sehar Kanjoo, Mehwish Bhatti, Jamshaid Ahmed
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 零剂量儿童是 2030 年免疫议程的优先事项,其定义是不存在第一剂含有白喉-破伤风-百日咳的疫苗。
设计与对象
我们分析了 2022 年第 1 周至 2026 年第 25 周流行病学记录中的 73,223 份 麻疹 记录中的 19,569 例实验室确诊且流行病学相关的 麻疹 病例。
核心方法
分析包括趋势检验、斯皮尔曼相关性和调整后的泊松回归。
主要结果
在 19,569 例病例中,9706 例(50%)是零剂量病例,其中 5740 例(59%)是 9 个月以下的婴儿。在 13,825 例符合 疫苗 资格的病例中,有 3963 例(29%;95% CI 28-29)是零剂量的:2022 年为 21%,2024 年为 40%,2026 年为 17%。
解释与创新
麻疹零剂量是指通过常规或运动免疫没有含有麻疹的剂量。病例分为 疫苗 不合格(9 个月以下)和合格(9 个月或以上)。
科研与公卫意义
District prioritisation should combine counts and proportions, not administrative coverage. Case fatality was 0.71% in zero-dose and 0.26% in vaccinated eligible cases (adjusted relative risk 2.65, 95% CI 1.52–4.60).
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性。由于该研究使用了实验室确诊病例和统计分析,因此证据强度很高。

Zero-dose children and measles risk in Punjab, Pakistan: magnitude, geographic distribution, and programmatic implications, 2022–2026

Authors: Muhammad Mehroz Saleem, Mufakker Mian, Marvi Sehar Kanjoo, Mehwish Bhatti, Jamshaid Ahmed
Original abstract (English)
Background Zero-dose children, defined by absence of a first diphtheria-tetanus-pertussis-containing dose, are the priority of the Immunization Agenda 2030. Measles cases include many unvaccinated children, but those too young for vaccination are rarely separated from those the programme could have reached. We describe the magnitude, age, and geographic distribution of measles zero-dose status among confirmed cases in Punjab, Pakistan. Methods We analysed 19,569 laboratory-confirmed and epidemiologically linked measles cases among 73,223 surveillance records, epidemiological week 1 of 2022 to week 25 of 2026. Measles zero-dose meant no measles-containing dose through routine or campaign immunization. Cases were split into vaccine-ineligible (under 9 months) and eligible (9 months or older). Analyses included trend tests, Spearman correlation, and adjusted Poisson regression. Results Of 19,569 cases, 9706 (50%) were zero-dose, and 5740 of these (59%) were infants under 9 months. Among 13,825 vaccine-eligible cases, 3963 (29%; 95% CI 28–29) were zero-dose: 21% in 2022, 40% in 2024, and 17% in 2026. Dera Ghazi Khan (59%) and Lodhran (52%) had the highest district shares, unrelated to administrative MCV1 coverage (rho = −0.24, p = 0.15). Case fatality was 0.71% in zero-dose and 0.26% in vaccinated eligible cases (adjusted relative risk 2.65, 95% CI 1.52–4.60). Conclusions About three in ten cases were too young for vaccination, and about three in ten of the rest had never received a measles dose. Cases over-represent unvaccinated children, so these proportions guide field investigation, not prevalence. District prioritisation should combine counts and proportions, not administrative coverage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Background Zero-dose children, defined by absence of a first diphtheria-tetanus-pertussis-containing dose, are the priority of the Immunization Agenda 2030.
Design and population
We analysed 19,569 laboratory-confirmed and epidemiologically linked measles cases among 73,223 surveillance records, epidemiological week 1 of 2022 to week 25 of 2026.
Core methods
Analyses included trend tests, Spearman correlation, and adjusted Poisson regression.
Main results
Of 19,569 cases, 9706 (50%) were zero-dose, and 5740 of these (59%) were infants under 9 months. Among 13,825 vaccine-eligible cases, 3963 (29%; 95% CI 28–29) were zero-dose: 21% in 2022, 40% in 2024, and 17% in 2026.
Interpretation and novelty
Measles zero-dose meant no measles-containing dose through routine or campaign immunization. Cases were split into vaccine-ineligible (under 9 months) and eligible (9 months or older).
Scientific and public-health significance
District prioritisation should combine counts and proportions, not administrative coverage. Case fatality was 0.71% in zero-dose and 0.26% in vaccinated eligible cases (adjusted relative risk 2.65, 95% CI 1.52–4.60).
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations. The evidence strength is high as the study used laboratory-confirmed cases and statistical analyses.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2025 年埃塞俄比亚西南部达乌罗地区 Tocha Woreda 15-36 个月 MCV1 疫苗接种儿童第二剂 麻疹 患病率 的利用情况及相关因素

Prevalence of second-dose measles vaccination utilization and associated factors among MCV1-vaccinated children aged 15–36 months in Tocha Woreda, Dawuro Zone, Southwest Ethiopia, 2025
作者： Asnake Abriham Debancho, Abdilaahi Yusuf Nuuh, Henok Ashenafi Serba, Eshetu Alemayehu Wordofa, Ayantu Kebede
摘要中文翻译背景 麻疹 是一种高度传染性的病毒性疾病,也是全球儿童发病和 死亡率 的主要原因。关于在埃塞俄比亚西南部使用第二剂含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 (MCV2) 及其相关因素的证据仍然有限。本研究调查了 Dawuro 地区 Tocha Woreda 的 MCV2 利用率以及与其吸收相关的因素。方法 对 585 名 15-36 个月大的儿童进行了一项基于社区的横断面研究,这些儿童接受了第一剂含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 (MCV1)。使用多阶段系统抽样方法选择参与者。数据是通过与母亲或照顾者进行面对面访谈而收集的,使用的是通过 Kobo Collect 移动应用程序管理的结构化调查问卷。使用社会科学统计软件包 (SPSS) 27.0 版对数据进行分析。描述性统计总结了参与者的特征。双变量逻辑回归确定了包含在多变量逻辑回归模型中的候选变量 (p < 0.25),以识别 MCV2 利用的独立预测因子。统计显着性定义为 p 值 <0.05。使用方差膨胀因子评估多重共线性,并使用 Hosmer-Lemeshow 拟合优度检验评估模型拟合度。结果 在纳入的 585 名儿童中(有效率:94.4%),67.2%(95% CI:63-71%)接受了第二剂 麻疹 疫苗。城市住宅[调整后优势比(AOR)= 3.43;95% 置信区间 (CI):1.74–6.44]、母亲或看护者受过大学或以上教育(AOR = 12.76;95% CI:4.39–20.64)、参加孕妇会议(AOR = 2.25;95% CI:1.11–4.56)、更短的 疫苗接种 等待时间(≤15 分钟: AOR = 4.28;95% CI:1.83-10.01;15-30 分钟:AOR = 3.33;95% CI:1.35-8.16)和良好的护理态度(AOR = 6.05;95% CI:2.96-12.35)与 MCV2 使用显着相关。结论 Tocha Woreda 中的 MCV2 利用率仍低于世界卫生组织 (WHO) 消除 麻疹 的目标。改善孕产妇教育、加强孕妇会议、减少 疫苗接种 站点的等待时间以及提高护理人员的态度对于提高 MCV2 覆盖率至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 是一种高度传染性的病毒性疾病,也是全球儿童发病和 死亡率 的主要原因。关于在埃塞俄比亚西南部使用第二剂含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 (MCV2) 及其相关因素的证据仍然有限。
设计与对象
在 585 名 15-36 个月大的儿童中进行了一项基于社区的横断面研究,这些儿童接受了第一剂含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 (MCV1)。使用多阶段系统抽样方法选择参与者。
核心方法
数据是通过与母亲或照顾者进行面对面访谈而收集的,使用的是通过 Kobo Collect 移动应用程序管理的结构化调查问卷。双变量逻辑回归确定了包含在多变量逻辑回归模型中的候选变量 (p < 0.25),以识别 MCV2 利用的独立预测因子。使用社会科学统计软件包 (SPSS) 27.0 版对数据进行分析。描述性统计总结了参与者的特征。使用方差膨胀因子评估多重共线性,并使用 Hosmer-Lemeshow 拟合优度检验评估模型拟合度。
主要结果
在纳入的 585 名儿童中(有效率:94.4%),67.2%(95% CI:63-71%)接受了第二剂 麻疹 疫苗。城市住宅[调整后优势比(AOR)= 3.43;95% 置信区间 (CI):1.74–6.44]、母亲或看护者受过大学或以上教育(AOR = 12.76;95% CI:4.39–20.64)、参加孕妇会议(AOR = 2.25;95% CI:1.11–4.56)、更短的 疫苗接种 等待时间(≤15 分钟: AOR = 4.28;95% CI:1.83-10.01;15-30 分钟:AOR = 3.33;95% CI:1.35-8.16)和良好的护理态度(AOR = 6.05;95% CI:2.96-12.35)与 MCV2 使用显着相关。
解释与创新
该研究确定了与 MCV2 使用显着相关的因素,包括城市居住、较高的孕产妇教育、参加孕妇会议、较短的等待时间和良好的护理人员态度。这些发现强调了解决这些因素以提高 疫苗接种 覆盖率的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
改善孕产妇教育、加强孕妇会议、减少 疫苗接种 站点的等待时间以及提高护理人员的态度对于提高 MCV2 覆盖率至关重要。这些发现可以为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息,以提高该地区的 麻疹 麻疹 率。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。由于依赖自我报告的数据和潜在的混杂因素,证据的强度中等。

Prevalence of second-dose measles vaccination utilization and associated factors among MCV1-vaccinated children aged 15–36 months in Tocha Woreda, Dawuro Zone, Southwest Ethiopia, 2025

Authors: Asnake Abriham Debancho, Abdilaahi Yusuf Nuuh, Henok Ashenafi Serba, Eshetu Alemayehu Wordofa, Ayantu Kebede
Original abstract (English)
Background Measles is a highly contagious viral disease and a leading cause of morbidity and mortality among children worldwide. Evidence regarding the utilization of the second dose of the measles-containing vaccine (MCV2) and its associated factors in Southwest Ethiopia remains limited. This study examined MCV2 utilization rates and the factors associated with its uptake in Tocha Woreda, Dawuro Zone. Methods A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 585 children aged 15–36 months who had received the first dose of the measles-containing vaccine (MCV1). Participants were selected using a multi-stage systematic sampling method. Data were collected through in-person interviews with mothers or caregivers, using a structured questionnaire administered via the Kobo Collect mobile application. Data were analyzed using Statistical Package for the Social Sciences (SPSS) version 27.0. Descriptive statistics summarized participant characteristics. Bi-variable logistic regression identified candidate variables ( p < 0.25) for inclusion in a multivariable logistic regression model to identify independent predictors of MCV2 utilization. Statistical significance was defined as a p -value of <0.05. Multicollinearity was assessed using variance inflation factors, and model fit was evaluated using the Hosmer–Lemeshow goodness-of-fit test. Results Among the 585 children included (response rate: 94.4%), 67.2% (95% CI: 63–71%) received the second dose of measles vaccine. Urban residence [adjusted odds ratio (AOR) = 3.43; 95% confidence interval (CI): 1.74–6.44], maternal or caregiver education at the college level or higher (AOR = 12.76; 95% CI: 4.39–20.64), participation in conferences for pregnant women (AOR = 2.25; 95% CI: 1.11–4.56), shorter vaccination waiting times (≤15 min: AOR = 4.28; 95% CI: 1.83–10.01; 15–30 min: AOR = 3.33; 95% CI: 1.35–8.16), and favorable caregiver attitudes (AOR = 6.05; 95% CI: 2.96–12.35) were significantly associated with MCV2 utilization. Conclusion MCV2 utilization in Tocha Woreda remains below the World Health Organization (WHO) target for measles elimination. Improving maternal education, strengthening conferences for pregnant women, reducing waiting times at vaccination sites, and enhancing caregiver attitudes are essential to increasing MCV2 coverage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles is a highly contagious viral disease and a leading cause of morbidity and mortality among children worldwide. Evidence regarding the utilization of the second dose of the measles-containing vaccine (MCV2) and its associated factors in Southwest Ethiopia remains limited.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 585 children aged 15–36 months who had received the first dose of the measles-containing vaccine (MCV1). Participants were selected using a multi-stage systematic sampling method.
Core methods
Data were collected through in-person interviews with mothers or caregivers, using a structured questionnaire administered via the Kobo Collect mobile application. Bi-variable logistic regression identified candidate variables (p < 0.25) for inclusion in a multivariable logistic regression model to identify independent predictors of MCV2 utilization. Data were analyzed using Statistical Package for the Social Sciences (SPSS) version 27.0. Descriptive statistics summarized participant characteristics. Multicollinearity was assessed using variance inflation factors, and model fit was evaluated using the Hosmer–Lemeshow goodness-of-fit test.
Main results
Among the 585 children included (response rate: 94.4%), 67.2% (95% CI: 63–71%) received the second dose of measles vaccine. Urban residence [adjusted odds ratio (AOR) = 3.43; 95% confidence interval (CI): 1.74–6.44], maternal or caregiver education at the college level or higher (AOR = 12.76; 95% CI: 4.39–20.64), participation in conferences for pregnant women (AOR = 2.25; 95% CI: 1.11–4.56), shorter vaccination waiting times (≤15 min: AOR = 4.28; 95% CI: 1.83–10.01; 15–30 min: AOR = 3.33; 95% CI: 1.35–8.16), and favorable caregiver attitudes (AOR = 6.05; 95% CI: 2.96–12.35) were significantly associated with MCV2 utilization.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified factors significantly associated with MCV2 utilization, including urban residence, higher maternal education, participation in pregnant women conferences, shorter waiting times, and favorable caregiver attitudes. These findings highlight the importance of addressing these factors to improve vaccination coverage.
Scientific and public-health significance
Improving maternal education, strengthening conferences for pregnant women, reducing waiting times at vaccination sites, and enhancing caregiver attitudes are essential to increasing MCV2 coverage. These findings can inform targeted public health interventions to improve measles vaccination rates in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on self-reported data and the potential for confounding factors.

麻疹 地理分布的反应扩散 SVEIR 模型分析

Analysis of a reaction-diffusion SVEIR model for measles geographic spread
作者： Mahmoud H. DarAssi, Yousef AbuHour, Areej Almuneef, Zuhur Alqahtani

Analysis of a reaction-diffusion SVEIR model for measles geographic spread

Authors: Mahmoud H. DarAssi, Yousef AbuHour, Areej Almuneef, Zuhur Alqahtani
Original abstract (English)
We formulate and analyze a deterministic reaction-diffusion SVEIR model for the geographic spread of measles, explicitly incorporating vaccination, vaccine efficacy, latency, recovery, demographic turnover, and spatial diffusion. The disease-free equilibrium and the basic reproduction number $$\mathcal {R}_0$$ are obtained using the next-generation matrix, and linear stability analysis identifies the invasion threshold and the critical spatial mode. Because the critical wavenumber is zero, a long-wavelength analysis is used to characterize slow spatial variation near the invasion front. A mathematical consistency check shows that the common-diffusion formulation does not support a finite-wavenumber Turing instability; the roll, square, and hexagonal modes are therefore presented only as generic pattern-theory modes rather than as patterns proved to arise from the present model. Numerical simulations illustrate spatiotemporal propagation and quantify the effects of vaccine efficacy, vaccination rate, and transmission intensity.

问题与背景
该研究旨在记录一名完全接种疫苗的医护人员在 麻疹 期间出现突破性 麻疹 的病例,强调此类病例在接种疫苗的个体中的临床相关性。麻疹 在全球范围内卷土重来的同时,还出现了包括医护人员在内的先前免疫个体出现突破性感染的报告。

During outbreaks, documenting laboratory-confirmed cases in vaccinated healthcare workers is clinically relevant. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to document a case of breakthrough measles in a fully vaccinated healthcare worker during an outbreak, highlighting the clinical relevance of such cases in vaccinated individuals. The global resurgence of measles has been accompanied by reports of breakthrough infections in previously immunized individuals, including healthcare personnel.
Design and population
This is a case report describing a 30-year-old male pediatrician with a documented complete vaccination history who developed measles after occupational exposure to an unvaccinated 6-year-old patient during a measles outbreak in Mexico. The study was conducted during an active outbreak in Mexico.
Core methods
The case was evaluated through clinical observation and laboratory testing, including real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction to detect measles virus RNA in a pharyngeal swab. The patient received supportive care and was hospitalized for 2 days, with follow-up for 2 weeks.
Main results
Measles virus RNA was detected by real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction in a pharyngeal swab. The patient developed fever and a cephalocaudal maculopapular rash, followed by progressive systemic and respiratory symptoms, including cough, pharyngitis, and mild pain on breathing. Oxygenation improved rapidly with supportive care, and no complications or secondary cases were documented.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret this case as a descriptive, laboratory-confirmed example of breakthrough measles in a fully vaccinated healthcare worker during an active outbreak. The case contributes to understanding the clinical spectrum of measles in vaccinated adults.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case highlights the importance of documenting laboratory-confirmed breakthrough measles in vaccinated healthcare workers during outbreaks, which can inform surveillance and the potential need for targeted booster strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the absence of pre-exposure measles serology and viral genotyping, which prevents establishing the exact mechanism of vaccine failure. The study provides a descriptive example but lacks broader epidemiologic data, resulting in moderate confidence.

Not Immune After All: Polymerase Chain Reaction–Confirmed Measles in a Presumed-Immune 70-Year-Old Returning Traveler

Authors: Agostina E. Velo, Gabrielle Leavitt, Joel Grimaldo Ochoa, Vani Gandhi, Yuji Yamada, Amy Rosenberg
Original abstract (English)
Measles is an increasingly important consideration in adults with febrile rash illness, particularly after international travel. We report a 70-year-old woman who developed measles following travel to Israel despite presumed immunity based on age and a reported history of childhood infection. She presented with fever, cough, and rash, followed by cephalocaudal progression of the rash, conjunctival injection, and oral lesions. Oropharyngeal polymerase chain reaction confirmed measles; serum IgG was negative. This case highlights limitations of presumed immunity and the importance of early diagnosis, isolation, and public health notification.

Hemagglutinin and fusion proteins precede measles virus ribonucleoproteins and marshal cell-to-cell spread in airway epithelium
作者： Stephanie E. Clark, Justin W. Kaufman, Kalpana Yadav, Roberto Cattaneo, Brajesh K. Singh, Patrick L. Sinn
摘要中文翻译摘要 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 是最具传染性的人类呼吸道病原体。为了了解它如何在气道中传播,我们感染了分化良好的人气道上皮细胞(HAE)的原代培养物。我们发现 MeV 附着蛋白(血凝素,H)、融合蛋白(F)和组装组织蛋白(基质,M)先于复制复合体蛋白到达新感染的细胞,并且独立于复制复合体蛋白。为了表征 MeV 传播的机制,我们生成了复制缺陷型腺病毒 媒介,它以多西环素诱导的方式表达 MeV M、F 和 H 蛋白。这种病毒 媒介 形成了感染中心,保留了与 MeV 感染类似的上皮功能。为了进一步剖析这个过程,我们生成了表达单个 MeV 蛋白的 Ad 媒介,称为 Ad-M、Ad-F 和 Ad-H。用单、双和三 Ad 媒介 组合转导 HAE 表明,Ad-F 和 Ad-H 对于形成 MeV 样感染中心是必要且充分的。MeV 通过 H 蛋白与粘附连接蛋白 nectin-4 结合进入气道上皮并在气道上皮内传播。为了破坏传播,我们在 Ad-H 媒介 中引入了点 突变 H Y543A,这是先前描述的抑制 nectin-4 结合的 突变。Ad-F和Ad-H Y543A的共转导没有导致MeV样感染中心的形成。最后,我们评估了 MeV 相关副粘病毒尼帕 (NiV) 附着蛋白和融合蛋白(分别为 G 和 F)是否支持细胞间传播。HAE 与 Ad-NiV-G 和 Ad-NiV-F 的共转导不会导致感染中心的形成,这表明膜融合装置功能与复制的解偶联是 MeV 包膜糖蛋白的独特特征。重要性 麻疹病毒 是一种高度传染性的病原体。我们在离体模型中描述了 麻疹病毒 在人体气道内细胞中快速传播的机制。我们表明,病毒膜融合装置的蛋白质,血凝素(H)和融合蛋白(F),独立于复制复合物的蛋白质移动并先到达新感染的细胞。因此,病毒蛋白传播按顺序组装和排列(即编组)。使用基于腺病毒的 媒介,我们发现 H 和 F 蛋白的共表达促进快速顺序传播到多个细胞和感染中心的形成。进一步阐明 麻疹病毒 肺部扩增和细胞间传播的机制可能有助于解释其高度传染性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹病毒 (MeV) 是最具传染性的人类呼吸道病原体。为了了解它如何在气道中传播,我们感染了分化良好的人气道上皮细胞(HAE)的原代培养物。
设计与对象
我们在离体模型中描述了 麻疹病毒 在人体气道内细胞中快速传播的机制。我们感染了人气道上皮细胞 (HAE) 的分化良好的原代培养物,以了解 MeV 如何在气道中传播。
核心方法
为了表征 MeV 传播的机制,我们生成了复制缺陷型腺病毒 媒介,它以多西环素诱导的方式表达 MeV M、F 和 H 蛋白。我们还生成了表达单个 MeV 蛋白的 Ad 媒介,称为 Ad-M、Ad-F 和 Ad-H,以进一步剖析这一过程。
主要结果
用单、双和三 Ad 媒介 组合转导 HAE 表明,Ad-F 和 Ad-H 对于形成 MeV 样感染中心是必要且充分的。HAE 与 Ad-NiV-G 和 Ad-NiV-F 的共转导不会导致感染中心的形成。
解释与创新
我们表明,病毒膜融合装置的蛋白质,血凝素(H)和融合蛋白(F),独立于复制复合物的蛋白质移动并先到达新感染的细胞。因此,病毒蛋白传播按顺序组装和排列(即编组)。
科研与公卫意义
进一步阐明 麻疹病毒 肺部扩增和细胞间传播的机制可能有助于解释其高度传染性。这可能为未来关于 麻疹病毒 传播 和预防策略的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性。该研究基于离体模型,不包括体内数据,这可能会限制普遍性。信心程度适中。

Hemagglutinin and fusion proteins precede measles virus ribonucleoproteins and marshal cell-to-cell spread in airway epithelium

Authors: Stephanie E. Clark, Justin W. Kaufman, Kalpana Yadav, Roberto Cattaneo, Brajesh K. Singh, Patrick L. Sinn
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Measles virus (MeV) is the most contagious human respiratory pathogen. To understand how it spreads in the airways, we infected well-differentiated primary cultures of human airway epithelial cells (HAE). We discovered that the MeV attachment protein (hemagglutinin, H), the fusion (F) protein, and the assembly organizer protein (matrix, M) reach newly infected cells ahead of, and independently from, the replicative complex proteins. To characterize the mechanisms of MeV spread, we generated a replication-deficient adenoviral vector expressing the MeV M, F, and H proteins in a doxycycline-inducible manner. This viral vector formed infectious centers that preserved epithelial function like those from MeV infection. To further dissect this process, we generated Ad vectors expressing individual MeV proteins, termed Ad-M, Ad-F, and Ad-H. Transduction of HAE with single, double, and triple Ad vector combinations revealed that Ad-F and Ad-H are necessary and sufficient to form MeV-like infectious centers. MeV enters and spreads within airway epithelia via H protein binding to adherens junction protein, nectin-4. To disrupt spread, we introduced the point mutation H Y543A , a mutation previously described to inhibit nectin-4 binding, in the Ad-H vector. Co-transduction of Ad-F and Ad-H Y543A did not result in MeV-like infectious center formation. Finally, we assessed whether the MeV-related paramyxovirus Nipah (NiV) attachment and fusion proteins (G and F, respectively) support cell-to-cell spread. Co-transduction of HAE with Ad-NiV-G and Ad-NiV-F did not result in infectious center formation, suggesting that decoupling of the membrane fusion apparatus function from replication is a unique feature of the MeV envelope glycoproteins. IMPORTANCE Measles virus is a highly contagious pathogen. We characterized a mechanism by which measles virus spreads rapidly through the cells that line the human airway in an ex vivo model. We show that the proteins of the viral membrane fusion apparatus, hemagglutinin (H) and fusion (F), move independently from and reach newly infected cells before the proteins of the replicative complex. Thus, viral protein spread is assembled and arranged in order (i.e., marshaled ). Using adenovirus-based vectors, we show that co-expression of H and F proteins promotes rapid sequential spread to multiple cells and the formation of infectious centers. Further elucidation of the mechanism of measles virus amplification and cell-to-cell spread within the lung may help explain its highly contagious nature.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles virus (MeV) is the most contagious human respiratory pathogen. To understand how it spreads in the airways, we infected well-differentiated primary cultures of human airway epithelial cells (HAE).
Design and population
We characterized a mechanism by which measles virus spreads rapidly through the cells that line the human airway in an ex vivo model. We infected well-differentiated primary cultures of human airway epithelial cells (HAE) to understand how MeV spreads in the airways.
Core methods
To characterize the mechanisms of MeV spread, we generated a replication-deficient adenoviral vector expressing the MeV M, F, and H proteins in a doxycycline-inducible manner. We also generated Ad vectors expressing individual MeV proteins, termed Ad-M, Ad-F, and Ad-H, to further dissect this process.
Main results
Transduction of HAE with single, double, and triple Ad vector combinations revealed that Ad-F and Ad-H are necessary and sufficient to form MeV-like infectious centers. Co-transduction of HAE with Ad-NiV-G and Ad-NiV-F did not result in infectious center formation.
Interpretation and novelty
We show that the proteins of the viral membrane fusion apparatus, hemagglutinin (H) and fusion (F), move independently from and reach newly infected cells before the proteins of the replicative complex. Thus, viral protein spread is assembled and arranged in order (i.e., marshaled).
Scientific and public-health significance
Further elucidation of the mechanism of measles virus amplification and cell-to-cell spread within the lung may help explain its highly contagious nature. This may inform future studies on measles virus transmission and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations. The study is based on an ex vivo model and does not include in vivo data, which may limit generalisability. Confidence is moderate.

Nectin-4 介导风疹病毒进入气道和胎盘细胞

Nectin-4 mediates rubella virus entry in airway and placental cells
作者： Yoshio Mori, Masafumi Sakata, Yuichiro Nakatsu, Takeshi Hori, Yoko Ino, Satoshi Suzuki, Mayuko Nishi, Miyuki Kawase, Masatoshi Kakizaki, Misato Nagai
摘要中文翻译摘要 风疹病毒 (RuV) 会引起风疹,如果在怀孕期间感染,则会引起先天性风疹综合征 (CRS)。尽管具有临床意义,但由于对 宿主 受体的了解有限,RuV 的细胞进入机制仍然知之甚少,这阻碍了 发病机制 的研究和治疗开发。在这里,我们通过基于邻近生物素标记的蛋白质组学结合 CRISPR/Cas9 验证,将 Nectin-4 确定为功能性 RuV 进入受体。值得注意的是,Nectin-4 与 麻疹病毒 共享,揭示了尽管病毒家族不同,但病毒进入过程中 麻疹病毒 趋同。Nectin-4 通过其胞外域直接与 RuV 包膜糖蛋白相互作用,在最初的钙依赖性病毒附着后促进受体介导的内吞作用。在极化的呼吸道上皮细胞中,Nectin-4 介导基底外侧病毒进入和随后子代病毒粒子的顶端释放,从而实现高效的呼吸道 传播。使用人胎盘类器官模型,我们证明合体滋养层是经胎盘 RuV 感染的主要目标,Nectin-4 阻断可显着抑制病毒进入。这些发现表明,Nectin-4 对于 RuV 的水平呼吸 传播 和垂直母胎 传播 至关重要。我们的结果提供了对 RuV 发病机制 的基本见解,并将 Nectin-4 确定为 CRS 的有前途的治疗靶点。重要的是,RuV 和 麻疹病毒 之间共享受体利用的发现为针对这两种病原体的综合消除策略开辟了新途径。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决对 麻疹病毒 细胞进入机制缺乏了解的问题,该机制阻碍了 麻疹病毒 研究和治疗开发。
设计与对象
该研究利用基于邻近生物素标记的蛋白质组学结合 CRISPR/Cas9 验证来鉴定 麻疹病毒 进入受体。它还包括人类胎盘类器官模型来检查经胎盘感染。
核心方法
该研究采用基于邻近生物素标记的蛋白质组学来识别潜在受体,并采用 CRISPR/Cas9 验证来确认其功能。人胎盘类器官模型用于评估病毒进入和 传播。
主要结果
Nectin-4 被鉴定为功能性 麻疹病毒 进入受体。在胎盘类器官中,Nectin-4 阻断可显着抑制病毒进入。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,Nectin-4 对于呼吸道和经胎盘 麻疹病毒 传播 都至关重要,为了解该病毒的 麻疹病毒 提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
Nectin-4作为受体的鉴定为开发靶向治疗和改进麻疹病毒预防和控制策略奠定了基础。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的证据,并且研究结果基于体外模型,可能无法完全代表体内条件。证据强度中等。

Nectin-4 mediates rubella virus entry in airway and placental cells

Authors: Yoshio Mori, Masafumi Sakata, Yuichiro Nakatsu, Takeshi Hori, Yoko Ino, Satoshi Suzuki, Mayuko Nishi, Miyuki Kawase, Masatoshi Kakizaki, Misato Nagai
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Rubella virus (RuV) causes rubella and, when acquired during pregnancy, congenital rubella syndrome (CRS). Despite its clinical significance, the cellular entry mechanism of RuV remains poorly characterized owing to limited knowledge of host receptors, hampering pathogenesis studies and therapeutic development. Here, we identify Nectin-4 as a functional RuV entry receptor through proximity biotin labeling-based proteomics coupled with CRISPR/Cas9 validation. Notably, Nectin-4 is shared with measles virus, revealing convergent evolution in viral entry despite the different viral families. Nectin-4 directly interacts with the RuV envelope glycoproteins through its ectodomain, facilitating receptor-mediated endocytosis following initial calcium-dependent viral attachment. In polarized respiratory epithelial cells, Nectin-4 mediates basolateral viral entry and subsequent apical release of progeny virions, enabling efficient respiratory transmission. Using human placental organoid models, we demonstrate that syncytiotrophoblasts serve as primary targets for transplacental RuV infection, with viral entry significantly inhibited by Nectin-4 blockade. These findings establish Nectin-4 as critical for both horizontal respiratory transmission and vertical maternal–fetal transmission of RuV. Our results provide fundamental insights into RuV pathogenesis and identify Nectin-4 as a promising therapeutic target for CRS. Importantly, the discovery of shared receptor utilization between RuV and measles virus opens new avenues for integrated elimination strategies targeting both pathogens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of understanding regarding the cellular entry mechanism of measles virus, which has hindered pathogenesis studies and therapeutic development.
Design and population
The research utilized proximity biotin labeling-based proteomics combined with CRISPR/Cas9 validation to identify the measles virus entry receptor. It also included human placental organoid models to examine transplacental infection.
Core methods
The study employed proximity biotin labeling-based proteomics to identify potential receptors and CRISPR/Cas9 validation to confirm their functionality. Human placental organoid models were used to assess viral entry and transmission.
Main results
Nectin-4 was identified as a functional measles virus entry receptor. In placental organoids, viral entry was significantly inhibited by Nectin-4 blockade.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal that Nectin-4 is critical for both respiratory and transplacental measles virus transmission, offering new insights into the virus's pathogenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The identification of Nectin-4 as a receptor provides a foundation for developing targeted therapies and improving strategies for measles virus prevention and control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence, and the findings are based on in vitro models, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The evidence strength is moderate.

孟加拉国的 麻疹:一场可以预防的危机

Measles in Bangladesh: A Preventable Crisis
作者： Md Kawsar Hossain
摘要中文翻译摘要不可用 J Com Med Col Teachers’ Asso 2026 年 7 月;30(2):72
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究针对孟加拉国正在发生的 麻疹 危机,强调需要了解该疾病的影响及其预防方面的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究是一篇社论,分析了孟加拉国的 麻疹 情况,重点关注该疾病的 麻疹 以及导致其在该地区持续存在的因素。
核心方法
该社论回顾了有关孟加拉国 麻疹、暴发、疫苗 覆盖范围和公共卫生应对措施的现有文献和报告,但没有详细说明具体方法或数据收集程序。
主要结果
社论指出,由于 麻疹 发病率低以及疾病 监测 的差距,麻疹 在孟加拉国仍然是一场可以预防的危机。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解读为呼吁采取行动,改进孟加拉国的 麻疹 控制策略,强调需要更好的 监测 和 疫苗接种 计划。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明了在孟加拉国加强 麻疹 监测 和免疫工作以预防 暴发 和减轻疾病负担的重要性。
局限与证据强度
证据基于仅抽象的审查,这限制了分析的深度和得出的结论的强度。

The study addresses the ongoing measles crisis in Bangladesh, highlighting the need to understand the disease's impact and the challenges in its prevention.

The study is an editorial analyzing the measles situation in Bangladesh, focusing on the disease's prevalence and the factors contributing to its persistence in the region.

麻疹 疫苗 的“97% 有效”评级对匹兹堡家长意味着什么

What the measles vaccine’s ‘97% effective’ rating means for Pittsburgh parents
作者： Rebecca Dawson
摘要中文翻译结果,包括阿勒格尼县在内的许多社区不再具有群体免疫力。根据该州编制的统计数据,宾夕法尼亚州正在经历三十多年来最严重的 麻疹 麻疹 麻疹,截至 2026 年,已有 1,400 多例确诊病例和 6 例与 麻疹 相关的死亡。每个新病例都会带来该社区中 麻疹 暴露的新警告。医院和医生办公室、杂货店和餐馆以及 K-12 学校和大学都曾发生过接触。暴露警报附带相同的说明:检查您的 MMR 疫苗 状态,并在接下来的三周内观察症状。人们被告知,两剂 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 麻疹 对预防 麻疹 的有效率为 97%。潜在的信息是,如果您接种了疫苗,您就会受到保护。MMR 疫苗 在预防疾病方面非常有效,但这并不能保证。人们可能听到 97% 的数字,并对另外 3% 感到好奇。父母问我:我应该担心吗?我如何知道我的孩子是否患有 麻疹?我需要 MMR 增强剂吗?最近,我十几岁的女儿给我发短信询问她的 MMR 疫苗 状态。她担心如果自己暴露了会发生什么。作为宾夕法尼亚州西北部的一名母亲和流行病学家,我可以回答这些问题。我同时成为了两者。我的女儿在我博士课程开始时出生,我在准备综合考试的同时给她大声朗读《流行病学》教科书。我在儿子的预产期那天进行了论文答辩。我是一个更好的母亲,因为我是一名流行病学家;我是一个更好的流行病学家,因为我是一个母亲。这些 麻疹 暴露警报是否会引起警报?每个公开的 麻疹 暴露警报中的建议与医生在患者可能已经接触过的情况下遵循的指导相匹配。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了宾夕法尼亚州 麻疹 暴发 的公共卫生问题,以及 MMR 疫苗 在预防 麻疹 方面的有效性,特别是考虑到 97% 的有效性评级及其对 疫苗接种 率低的社区的影响。
设计与对象
该研究基于宾夕法尼亚州的公共卫生统计数据和流行病学观察,重点关注阿勒格尼县和其他受影响地区儿童和成人的 麻疹 病例和 疫苗接种 比率。
核心方法
该研究依靠公共卫生记录、暴露警报和流行病学数据来评估 疫苗接种 率和 麻疹 传播 动态,而无需进行新的实验或详细的统计分析。
主要结果
宾夕法尼亚州 1,400 多例 麻疹 确诊病例中有 4 例是接种疫苗的个体,阿勒格尼县 93.8% 的儿童已完全接种 麻疹 疫苗,一些社区低于群体免疫所需的 95% 阈值。
解释与创新
作者将 MMR 疫苗 97% 的有效性解释为提供了强大的保护,但不是绝对的免疫力,并强调了维持高 疫苗接种 率以预防 暴发 的重要性,并指出即使接种疫苗的个体在极少数情况下也可能感染 麻疹。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为 麻疹 预防的公共卫生策略提供了信息,强调需要保持 麻疹 的高覆盖率,以实现群体免疫并降低 麻疹 的风险,特别是在吸收率较低的社区。
局限与证据强度
证据基于公共卫生统计数据,不包括详细的实验数据或随机试验,这将结论的强度限制在中等水平。

What the measles vaccine’s ‘97% effective’ rating means for Pittsburgh parents

Authors: Rebecca Dawson
Original abstract (English)
As a result, many communities, including Allegheny County, no longer have herd immunity. Pennsylvania is experiencing its worst measles outbreak in more than three decades, with more than 1,400 confirmed cases and six measles-associated deaths so far in 2026, according to statistics compiled by the state. Each new case brings a new warning of a measles exposure in that community. Exposures have occurred at hospitals and doctors’ offices, grocery stores and restaurants, and K-12 schools and universities. Exposure alerts come with the same instructions: Check your MMR vaccine status, and watch for symptoms for the next three weeks. People are told that two doses of the measles, mumps and rubella vaccine are 97% effective at preventing measles. The underlying message is that if you’re vaccinated, you’re protected. The MMR vaccine is highly effective at preventing disease, but it isn’t a guarantee. People may hear that 97% figure and wonder about the other 3%. Parents ask me: Should I be worried? How do I know if my kid has measles? Do I need an MMR booster? Most recently, my teenage daughter texted me to ask about her MMR vaccine status. She was worried about what would happen if she were exposed. These are questions I can answer as both a mother and an epidemiologist in northwestern Pennsylvania. I became both at once. My daughter was born at the start of my doctoral program, and I read epidemiology textbooks aloud to her while studying for my comprehensive exams. I defended my dissertation on my son’s due date. I’m a better mother because I’m an epidemiologist, and a better epidemiologist because I’m a mom. Are these measles exposure alerts cause for alarm? The advice in each public measles exposure alert matches the guidance doctors follow when a patient may have been...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the public health concern of measles outbreaks in Pennsylvania and the effectiveness of the MMR vaccine in preventing measles, particularly in light of the 97% effectiveness rating and its implications for communities with low vaccination rates.
Design and population
The study is based on public health statistics and epidemiological observations from Pennsylvania, focusing on measles cases and vaccination rates among children and adults in Allegheny County and other affected areas.
Core methods
The study relies on public health records, exposure alerts, and epidemiological data to assess vaccination rates and measles transmission dynamics, without conducting new experiments or detailed statistical analyses.
Main results
Four of the over 1,400 confirmed measles cases in Pennsylvania were among vaccinated individuals, and 93.8% of children in Allegheny County are fully vaccinated against measles, with some neighborhoods below the 95% threshold needed for herd immunity.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the 97% effectiveness of the MMR vaccine as providing strong protection, but not absolute immunity, and highlight the importance of maintaining high vaccination rates to prevent outbreaks, noting that even vaccinated individuals can contract measles in rare cases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform public health strategies for measles prevention, emphasizing the need for maintaining high vaccination coverage to achieve herd immunity and reduce the risk of outbreaks, particularly in communities with lower uptake.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on public health statistics and does not include detailed experimental data or randomized trials, which limits the strength of the conclusions to a moderate level.

疫苗 相关荨麻疹的流行病学特征和临床结果:疫苗 不良事件报告系统数据库(1990-2025)的回顾性横断面分析。

Epidemiological Characteristics and Clinical Outcomes of Vaccine-associated Urticaria: A Retrospective Cross-sectional Analysis of the Vaccine Adverse Event Reporting System Database (1990-2025).
作者： Chetan Chandrashekar, Poornima Andal Peachiappan, Herrieth Jandwa, Mansiben Patel, Amardeepsinh Jadeja
摘要中文翻译简介:荨麻疹是 疫苗接种 后常见的皮肤不良事件,其范围可能从轻微的自限性反应到更严重的超敏反应表现。监测 疫苗 相关荨麻疹对于持续的 疫苗 安全性和药物警戒非常重要。然而,评估人口特征、疫苗 关联、发病间隔和严重程度模式的大规模基于人群的分析仍然有限。目的:本研究的目的是分析 1990 年至 2025 年间 疫苗 不良事件报告系统 (VAERS) 数据库中的荨麻疹报告,并评估人口特征、疫苗 类型分布、发病间隔、时间趋势和严重性模式。方法:使用 VAERS 数据库的数据进行回顾性观察研究。包括 1990 年 1 月至 2025 年 3 月期间提交的荨麻疹报告。分析的变量包括年龄、性别、疫苗 类型、发病间隔和严重程度分类。使用频率和百分比生成描述性统计数据,并在严肃报告和非严肃报告之间进行比较分析。结果:研究期间共发现 61,996 份荨麻疹报告。大多数报告发生在 18-49 岁的成年人和女性中。大多数报告被归类为不严重,并且发生在疫苗接种后早期。COVID-19 疫苗 报告比例最高,其次是流感和 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 疫苗。时间分析表明,2021 年报告数量显着增加,随后呈下降趋势。比较分析确定了严重报告和非严重报告之间人口统计学和临床​​特征的差异。结论:荨麻疹是 疫苗接种 治疗后经常报告的不良事件,具有可识别的人口统计学、时间和临床模式。大多数报告都是不严重的,并且发生在 疫苗接种 后不久,尽管严重报告和不严重报告之间存在差异。需要持续的药物警戒和未来的研究结合详细的临床数据,以更好地了解与 疫苗 相关荨麻疹相关的因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在分析 VAERS 数据库中的荨麻疹报告,以评估人口特征、疫苗 类型分布、发病间隔、时间趋势和严重性模式。荨麻疹是 疫苗接种 后常见的皮肤不良事件,其范围可能从轻微的自限性反应到更严重的过敏表现。监测 疫苗 相关荨麻疹对于持续的 疫苗 安全性和药物警戒非常重要。
设计与对象
使用 VAERS 数据库的数据进行了一项回顾性观察研究,包括 1990 年 1 月至 2025 年 3 月期间提交的荨麻疹报告。该研究包括 61,996 份荨麻疹报告,并分析了年龄、性别、疫苗 类型、发病间隔和严重程度分类等变量。
核心方法
使用频率和百分比生成描述性统计数据,并在严肃报告和非严肃报告之间进行比较分析。该研究使用了来自 VAERS 数据库的数据,该数据库是一个被动的 监测 系统,用于监测 疫苗接种 后的不良事件。
主要结果
研究期间共发现 61,996 份荨麻疹报告。大多数报告发生在 18-49 岁的成年人和女性中。大多数报告被归类为不严重,并且发生在疫苗接种后早期。COVID-19 疫苗 报告比例最高,其次是流感和 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 疫苗。时间分析表明,2021 年报告数量显着增加,随后呈下降趋势。比较分析确定了严重报告和非严重报告之间人口统计学和临床​​特征的差异。
解释与创新
研究发现,荨麻疹是 疫苗接种 后经常报告的不良事件,具有可识别的人口、时间和临床模式。大多数报告都是不严重的,并且发生在 疫苗接种 后不久,尽管严重报告和不严重报告之间存在差异。需要持续的药物警戒和未来的研究结合详细的临床数据,以更好地了解与 疫苗 相关荨麻疹相关的因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为正在进行的 疫苗 安全性和药物警戒工作提供信息。该研究强调了监测 疫苗 相关荨麻疹并了解其模式对于改善 疫苗 安全和公共卫生的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到 VAERS 数据库的被动 监测 性质的限制,这可能会引入报告偏差。该研究不提供因果关系,本质上是观察性的。由于观察设计和潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Epidemiological Characteristics and Clinical Outcomes of Vaccine-associated Urticaria: A Retrospective Cross-sectional Analysis of the Vaccine Adverse Event Reporting System Database (1990-2025).

Authors: Chetan Chandrashekar, Poornima Andal Peachiappan, Herrieth Jandwa, Mansiben Patel, Amardeepsinh Jadeja
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Urticaria is a commonly reported cutaneous adverse event following vaccination, which may range from mild self-limiting reactions to more severe hypersensitivity manifestations. Monitoring vaccine-associated urticaria is important for ongoing vaccine safety surveillance and pharmacovigilance. However, large-scale population-based analyses evaluating demographic characteristics, vaccine associations, onset intervals, and severity patterns remain limited. AIM: The aim of the study was to analyze reports of urticaria in the Vaccine Adverse Event Reporting System (VAERS) database between 1990 and 2025 and evaluate demographic characteristics, vaccine type distribution, onset intervals, temporal trends, and seriousness patterns. METHODOLOGY: A retrospective observational study was conducted using data from the VAERS database. Reports of urticaria submitted between January 1990 and March 2025 were included. The variables analyzed included age, sex, vaccine type, onset interval, and seriousness classification. Descriptive statistics were generated using frequencies and percentages, and comparative analyses were performed between serious and nonserious reports. RESULTS: A total of 61,996 urticaria reports were identified during the study period. Most reports occurred among adults aged 18-49 years and females. Most reports were classified as nonserious and occurred within the early postvaccination period. COVID-19 vaccines accounted for the highest proportion of reports, followed by influenza and measles, mumps, and rubella vaccines. Temporal analysis demonstrated a marked increase in reporting during 2021, followed by declining trends thereafter. Comparative analysis identified differences in demographic and clinical characteristics between serious and nonserious reports. CONCLUSION: Urticaria is a frequently reported adverse event following vaccination, with identifiable demographic, temporal, and clinical patterns. Most reports were nonserious and occurred shortly after vaccination, although differences were observed between serious and nonserious reports. Continued pharmacovigilance and future studies incorporating detailed clinical data are needed to better understand factors associated with vaccine-related urticaria.

Question and background
The study aimed to analyze reports of urticaria in the VAERS database to evaluate demographic characteristics, vaccine type distribution, onset intervals, temporal trends, and seriousness patterns. Urticaria is a commonly reported cutaneous adverse event following vaccination, which may range from mild self-limiting reactions to more severe hypersensitivity manifestations. Monitoring vaccine-associated urticaria is important for ongoing vaccine safety surveillance and pharmacovigilance.

A retrospective observational study was conducted using data from the VAERS database, including reports of urticaria submitted between January 1990 and March 2025. The study included 61,996 urticaria reports and analyzed variables such as age, sex, vaccine type, onset interval, and seriousness classification.

Descriptive statistics were generated using frequencies and percentages, and comparative analyses were performed between serious and nonserious reports. The study used data from the VAERS database, which is a passive surveillance system for monitoring adverse events following vaccination.

A total of 61,996 urticaria reports were identified during the study period. Most reports occurred among adults aged 18-49 years and females. Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform ongoing vaccine safety surveillance and pharmacovigilance efforts. The study highlights the importance of monitoring vaccine-associated urticaria and understanding its patterns to improve vaccine safety and public health.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the passive surveillance nature of the VAERS database, which may introduce reporting biases. The study does not provide causal relationships and is observational in nature. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational design and potential for confounding factors.

Childhood vaccination at a crossroads: rebuilding confidence, overcoming inequities, and translating scientific advances into global health gains.
作者： Seif S Al-Abri, Ben J. Marais, Markus J. Maeurer, Peter Mwaba, Giuseppe Ippolito, Sam Allen, Alimuddin Zumla

Childhood vaccination at a crossroads: rebuilding confidence, overcoming inequities, and translating scientific advances into global health gains.

Authors: Seif S Al-Abri, Ben J. Marais, Markus J. Maeurer, Peter Mwaba, Giuseppe Ippolito, Sam Allen, Alimuddin Zumla
Original abstract (English)
The resurgence of measles is a warning that decades of progress in childhood immunisation cannot be taken for granted. A Global Burden of Disease (GBD) 2023 analysis covering 204 countries and territories estimates 15.6 million measles cases and 118,000 deaths among children younger than five years in 2023. Following a decline of approximately 79% in estimated cases between 1990 and 2019, cases increased by 40% between 2019 and 2023. First-dose measles-containing vaccine (MCV1) coverage was estimated at 81.0% and second-dose (MCV2) coverage at 71.4% in 2023 [1] These are modelled estimates with substantial uncertainty, not surveillance case counts.

马里的童年 疫苗接种:2023-2024 年人口和健康调查中受冲突影响地区和非冲突地区之间的决定因素和差异。

Childhood vaccination in Mali: Determinants and differences between conflict-affected and non-conflict regions from the 2023-2024 Demographic and Health Survey.
作者： Rodolfo Rossi, Abdullah Fannir, Amalid Chebly, Aly Ouattara, Kurt Barbé, Ives Hubloue
摘要中文翻译背景:武装冲突可能会扰乱免疫服务并增加儿童 疫苗接种 的障碍。本研究调查了马里完整基础童年 疫苗接种 的决定因素,并评估了这些决定因素在受冲突影响地区和非冲突地区之间是否存在差异。方法:我们对 2023-2024 年马里人口和健康调查进行了二次分析,其中包括 2859 名 12-23 个月大的儿童。完全基本疫苗接种被定义为接受卡介苗疫苗、三剂五价疫苗、三剂口服脊髓灰质炎疫苗(不包括出生剂量)和麻疹 疫苗。调查加权逻辑回归模型在全国范围内分别针对受冲突影响和非冲突地区进行拟合。测试了冲突状态和选定的解释变量之间的相互作用。结果:疫苗接种 的基本基本覆盖率为 51.87%。受冲突影响地区和非冲突地区的覆盖范围没有显着差异(50.08% vs 52.64%;p = 0.467),调整后冲突状态与 疫苗接种 不独立相关。产前护理就诊率和设施分娩始终与较高的完全 疫苗接种 几率相关。在受冲突影响的地区,媒体曝光和距医疗机构的感知距离较短也与 疫苗接种 的使用率较高有关。观察到冲突状态和距医疗机构的距离之间存在显着的交互作用 (p = 0.01)。结论:马里的儿童 疫苗接种 覆盖率仍然不理想。尽管冲突地区和非冲突地区的总体覆盖范围相似,但决定因素因背景而异,凸显需要制定量身定制的策略来提高 疫苗 的采用率。
