Childhood vaccination in Mali: Determinants and differences between conflict-affected and non-conflict regions from the 2023-2024 Demographic and Health Survey.

Authors: Rodolfo Rossi, Abdullah Fannir, Amalid Chebly, Aly Ouattara, Kurt Barbé, Ives Hubloue
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Armed conflict may disrupt immunization services and increase barriers to childhood vaccination. This study examined determinants of full basic childhood vaccination in Mali and assessed whether they differed between conflict-affected and non-conflict regions. METHODS: We conducted a secondary analysis of the 2023-2024 Mali Demographic and Health Survey, including 2859 children aged 12-23 months. Full basic vaccination was defined as receipt of Bacille Calmette-Guérin vaccine, three doses of pentavalent vaccine, three doses of oral polio vaccine (excluding the birth dose), and measles vaccine. Survey-weighted logistic regression models were fitted nationally and separately for conflict-affected and non-conflict regions. Interactions between conflict status and selected explanatory variables were tested. RESULTS: Full basic vaccination coverage was 51.87%. Coverage did not differ significantly between conflict-affected and non-conflict regions (50.08% vs 52.64%; p = 0.467), and conflict status was not independently associated with vaccination after adjustment. Antenatal care attendance and facility-based delivery were consistently associated with higher odds of full vaccination. In conflict-affected regions, media exposure and shorter perceived distance to health facilities were also associated with higher vaccination uptake. A significant interaction was observed between conflict status and distance to health facility (p = 0.01). CONCLUSIONS: Childhood vaccination coverage in Mali remains suboptimal. Although overall coverage was similar across conflict and non-conflict regions, determinants differed by context, highlighting the need for tailored strategies to improve vaccine uptake.

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study.

儿童 B-ALL 中 990 例 blinatumomab 免疫治疗后的感染并发症 - 来自印度一家大型三级癌症护理中心的真实世界分析

990 Infectious complications following blinatumomab immunotherapy in paediatric B-ALL- A real-world analysis from a large tertiary cancer care centre in India
作者： Subham Kumar, Shyam Srinivasan, Chetan Anil Dhamne, Nirmalya Roy Moulik, Akanksha Chichra, Sweta Rajpal, Gaurav Chatterjee, Prashant Ramesh Tembhare, Nikhil V. Patkar, Dhanlaxmi Lalit Shetty
摘要中文翻译背景 博纳吐单抗的作用机制会诱导 T 细胞活化和低丙种球蛋白血症,从而使患者易于感染。在中低收入国家,不同的环境病原体暴露、资源限制和基线营养变化可能会放大这些风险。有关 blinatumomab 在中低收入国家中感染情况的真实数据仍然很少。我们的目标是在资源有限的环境中描述 发病率、微生物谱和 blinatumomab 治疗期间感染并发症的临床影响。方法我们对 2021 年 5 月至 2026 年 3 月在孟买 TMH 接受 blinatumomab 的复发/难治性 B-ALL 儿科患者进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究。记录从开始使用 blinatumomab 到治疗后 60 天的感染发作情况,并使用 CTCAE v 5.0 进行分级。分析了伴随细胞因子释放综合征(CRS)、治疗后 IgG 水平和生存结果的临床相关性。结果 有 105 名患者符合分析条件,其中 55 名(52.4%)在开始 治疗 后 60 天内出现发热发作。几乎所有这些患者 (54/55) 均出现感染,并有 46 例 (85%) 出现全身症状,8 例 (15%) 出现局部症状。在后一组中,一半(4/8)是由于中心线部位相关感染。42例全身感染中,最常见的部位为呼吸道(n = 6,38.1%)和血流(n = 15,35.7%)。其中1例为结肠炎相关病毒血症,1例为急性化脓性中耳炎(2.4%),其余10例(23.8%)原发部位未明确。病毒病因在该亚群中占主导地位,占事件的 50% (n = 21),其中 麻疹 是最常见的分离病原体(图 1)。所有死亡均由病毒病原体驱动,疫苗 可预防疾病如 麻疹 是 80% 病例 (n = 4/5) 死亡的主要原因,原因是 麻疹 脑炎,其中 1 例死亡是副流感引起的肺炎。结论 感染在接受博纳吐单抗治疗的儿童 B-ALL 患者中很常见,并且可能发生在超过一半的病人。系统性病毒病原体的频率高于细菌病原体,并且几乎完全是 死亡率 的原因。伴随的 CRS 的临床严重程度(≥ 2 级)和较低的 IgG 水平与感染易感性相关。观察到的所有 死亡率 都是 疫苗 可预防的疾病。虽然社区 疫苗接种 率下降可能是造成局部 死亡率 的原因,但包括流感 死亡率 在内的有针对性的干预措施可能有助于缓解 死亡率。摘要 990 图 1 系统性病原体和感染相关的 死亡率。CRS与感染的关联摘要990 图2CRS严重程度与60天感染的关联发病率
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Blinatumomab 的作用机制会诱导 T 细胞活化和低丙种球蛋白血症,从而使患者容易感染。在中低收入国家,不同的环境病原体暴露、资源限制和基线营养变化可能会放大这些风险。
设计与对象
我们对 2021 年 5 月至 2026 年 3 月期间在孟买 TMH 接受 blinatumomab 治疗的复发/难治性 B-ALL 儿科患者进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究。有关 blinatumomab 在中低收入国家中感染情况的真实数据仍然很少。
核心方法
我们的目标是描述在资源有限的环境中博纳吐单抗治疗期间感染并发症的 发病率、微生物谱和临床影响。记录从开始使用 blinatumomab 到治疗后 60 天的感染发作情况,并使用 CTCAE v 5.0 进行分级。
主要结果
有 105 名患者符合分析条件,其中 55 名患者(52.4%)在开始 治疗 后 60 天内出现发热发作。几乎所有这些患者 (54/55) 均出现感染,并有 46 例 (85%) 出现全身症状,8 例 (15%) 出现局部症状。
解释与创新
在接受 blinatumomab 治疗的儿童 B-ALL 患者中,感染发作非常普遍,病毒病因占感染发作的 50% (n = 21)。伴随的 CRS 的临床严重程度(≥ 2 级)和较低的 IgG 水平与感染易感性相关。
科研与公卫意义
系统性病毒病原体的频率高于细菌病原体,并且几乎完全是 死亡率 的原因。包括流感 死亡率 在内的有针对性的干预措施可能有助于减轻 死亡率 的影响。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性或证据的强度。然而,该研究基于来自单个三级癌症护理中心的真实数据,这可能会限制普遍性。

990 Infectious complications following blinatumomab immunotherapy in paediatric B-ALL- A real-world analysis from a large tertiary cancer care centre in India

Authors: Subham Kumar, Shyam Srinivasan, Chetan Anil Dhamne, Nirmalya Roy Moulik, Akanksha Chichra, Sweta Rajpal, Gaurav Chatterjee, Prashant Ramesh Tembhare, Nikhil V. Patkar, Dhanlaxmi Lalit Shetty
Original abstract (English)
Background Blinatumomab’s mechanism of action induces T-cell activation and hypogammaglobulinemia which can predispose patients to infections. In LMICs, distinct environmental pathogen exposures, resource constraints, and baseline nutritional variations may amplify these risks. Real-world data regarding the infectious profile of blinatumomab in LMICs remains scarce. We aim to characterize the incidence, microbiological-spectrum, and clinical impact of infectious-complications during blinatumomab-therapy in a resource-constrained setting.Methods We conducted a retrospective cohort study of pediatric patients with relapsed/refractory B-ALL who received blinatumomab between May 2021 and March 2026 at TMH, Mumbai. Infectious episodes were captured from the initiation of blinatumomab until 60 days post-therapy and graded using CTCAE v 5.0. Clinical correlations with concomitant Cytokine Release Syndrome (CRS), Post therapy IgG Levels, and survival outcomes were analyzed.Results There were 105 patients eligible for analysis, of which 55 (52.4%) developed a febrile episode within 60 days of initiating treatment. Almost all of these (54/55) had infections and presented with systemic features in 46 (85%), and local manifestations in 8 (15%). In the latter group, half (4/8) were due to Central-line site related infection. Amongst 42 episodes of systemic infections, the most common sites were respiratory tract (n = 6,38.1%) and the bloodstream(n = 15,35.7%).There was one case of colitis-associated viraemia, one with acute suppurative otitis media (2.4%),while the primary anatomic site remained unspecified in the remaining 10 cases (23.8%). Viral etiologies were highly predominant in this subset, accounting for 50% (n = 21) of the episodes, with Measles being the most frequently isolated pathogen ( figure 1). All deaths were driven by viral pathogens, with vaccine-preventable diseases like Measles being the primary cause of death in 80% of cases (n = 4/5),due to Measles Encephalitis, and one death being due to Parainfluenzae-induced pneumonia.Conclusions Infections are common in pediatric B-ALL patients treated with blinatumomab and may occur in more than half the patients. Systemic viral pathogens predominate over bacterial in frequency and are almost exclusively responsible for mortality. Clinical severity of concomitant CRS (≥ Grade 2) and lower IgG levels correlate with infectious vulnerability. All mortality observed was in vaccine-preventable diseases. While falling community vaccination rates may be responsible for localized outbreaks, targeted interventions including influenza vaccinations may help mitigate mortality.Abstract 990 Figure 1Systemic pathogen and infection related mortality. Association of CRS with infectionAbstract 990 Figure 2Association of CRS severity with 60-day infection incidence
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Blinatumomab’s mechanism of action induces T-cell activation and hypogammaglobulinemia which can predispose patients to infections. In LMICs, distinct environmental pathogen exposures, resource constraints, and baseline nutritional variations may amplify these risks.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective cohort study of pediatric patients with relapsed/refractory B-ALL who received blinatumomab between May 2021 and March 2026 at TMH, Mumbai. Real-world data regarding the infectious profile of blinatumomab in LMICs remains scarce.
Core methods
We aim to characterize the incidence, microbiological-spectrum, and clinical impact of infectious-complications during blinatumomab-therapy in a resource-constrained setting. Infectious episodes were captured from the initiation of blinatumomab until 60 days post-therapy and graded using CTCAE v 5.0.
Main results
There were 105 patients eligible for analysis, of which 55 (52.4%) developed a febrile episode within 60 days of initiating treatment. Almost all of these (54/55) had infections and presented with systemic features in 46 (85%), and local manifestations in 8 (15%).
Interpretation and novelty
Infectious episodes were highly prevalent in pediatric B-ALL patients treated with blinatumomab, with viral etiologies accounting for 50% (n = 21) of the episodes. Clinical severity of concomitant CRS (≥ Grade 2) and lower IgG levels correlate with infectious vulnerability.
Scientific and public-health significance
Systemic viral pathogens predominate over bacterial in frequency and are almost exclusively responsible for mortality. Targeted interventions including influenza vaccinations may help mitigate mortality.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the specific limitations of the study design or the strength of the evidence. However, the study is based on real-world data from a single tertiary cancer care centre, which may limit generalisability.

使用 基因组 全非 CG 甲基化模式对神经元 DNA 进行超灵敏检测

Ultrasensitive detection of neuronal DNA using genome-wide non-CG methylation patterns
作者： Netanel Loyfer, Amit Horn, Shmuel Hart, Jonathan Rosenski, Sheina Piyanzin, Judith Magenheim, Ofra Sabag, Adi Avniel Aran, Yshia Langer, Natalie Barsoum
摘要中文翻译摘要 基于甲基化的液体活检可以识别循环游离 DNA (cfDNA) 的组织起源。这些检测灵敏度的一个主要限制是细胞类型特异性 CpG 标记的数量有限。在这里,我们利用神经元中高丰度的非 CpG (CpH) 甲基化来实现神经元 DNA 的超灵敏检测。我们发现神经元的 CpH 甲基化程度比其他组织高 100 倍,其中明显的 CpH 甲基化很大程度上反映了实验假象。使用计算标准来识别携带甲基化 CpA 位点簇的 cfDNA 分子,我们检测到比例低至百万分之 20 的神经元 DNA。对 163 名个体的 231 个样本的分析显示,健康受试者血浆中的神经元 cfDNA 极少;患有 麻疹 或急性神经系统疾病的患者体内水平升高;脑死亡供体中的水平惊人地高,表明神经元正在广泛死亡;以及神经重症监护病房患者脑脊液中的最高水平。这些发现确立了非 CpG 甲基化的 基因组 全片段水平分析作为基于血液的脑损伤和疾病监测的超灵敏方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决当前基于甲基化的液体活检在识别循环游离 DNA (cfDNA) 组织起源方面的局限性,特别是在检测神经元 DNA 方面。背景强调这些测定灵敏度的主要限制是细胞类型特异性 CpG 标记的数量有限。
设计与对象
该研究分析了来自 163 名个体的 231 个样本,包括健康受试者、麻疹 患者、患有急性神经系统疾病的患者、脑死亡捐献者以及神经重症监护病房的患者。重点是测量这些不同人群的神经元 cfDNA 水平。
核心方法
研究人员使用计算标准来识别携带甲基化 CpA 位点簇的 cfDNA 分子,从而能够检测比例低至百万分之 20 的神经元 DNA。该方法用于分析样品。
主要结果
分析显示,健康受试者血浆中的神经元 cfDNA 含量极低,麻疹 或急性神经系统疾病患者的神经元 cfDNA 水平升高,脑死亡捐献者的水平惊人地高,而神经重症监护病房患者的脑脊液中的水平最高。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,基因组 全范围、非 CpG 甲基化的片段水平分析可以作为基于血液的脑损伤和疾病监测的超灵敏方法,为检测神经元 DNA 提供一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对于开发更灵敏的神经系统疾病和脑损伤诊断工具具有重要意义,有可能改善临床环境中的早期检测和监测。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对计算标准的依赖和缺乏直接病毒检测方法而受到限制。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它基于一组明确的样本和清晰、可量化的结果。

Ultrasensitive detection of neuronal DNA using genome-wide non-CG methylation patterns

Authors: Netanel Loyfer, Amit Horn, Shmuel Hart, Jonathan Rosenski, Sheina Piyanzin, Judith Magenheim, Ofra Sabag, Adi Avniel Aran, Yshia Langer, Natalie Barsoum
Original abstract (English)
Summary Methylation-based liquid biopsies can identify the tissue origins of circulating cell-free DNA (cfDNA). A major constraint on the sensitivity of these assays is the limited number of cell-type-specific CpG markers. Here we exploit the high abundance of non-CpG (CpH) methylation in neurons to enable ultrasensitive detection of neuronal DNA. We show that neurons carry 100-fold higher CpH methylation than other tissues, in which apparent CpH methylation largely reflects experimental artifacts. Using a computational criterion to identify cfDNA molecules carrying clusters of methylated CpA sites, we detect neuronal DNA at proportions as low as 20 parts per million. Analysis of 231 samples from 163 individuals revealed minimal neuronal cfDNA in the plasma of healthy subjects; elevated levels in patients with measles or acute neurological conditions; strikingly high levels in brain-dead donors, indicating extensive ongoing neuronal death; and the highest levels in the cerebrospinal fluid of patients in a neurological intensive care unit. These findings establish genome-wide, fragment-level analysis of non-CpG methylation as an ultrasensitive approach for blood-based monitoring of brain injury and disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of current methylation-based liquid biopsies in identifying the tissue origins of circulating cell-free DNA (cfDNA), particularly in the context of detecting neuronal DNA. The background highlights that a major constraint on the sensitivity of these assays is the limited number of cell-type-specific CpG markers.
Design and population
The study analyzed 231 samples from 163 individuals, including healthy subjects, patients with measles, patients with acute neurological conditions, brain-dead donors, and patients in a neurological intensive care unit. The focus was on measuring neuronal cfDNA levels across these diverse populations.
Core methods
The researchers used a computational criterion to identify cfDNA molecules carrying clusters of methylated CpA sites, enabling the detection of neuronal DNA at proportions as low as 20 parts per million. This method was applied to analyze the samples.
Main results
Analysis revealed minimal neuronal cfDNA in the plasma of healthy subjects, elevated levels in patients with measles or acute neurological conditions, strikingly high levels in brain-dead donors, and the highest levels in the cerebrospinal fluid of patients in a neurological intensive care unit.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that genome-wide, fragment-level analysis of non-CpG methylation can serve as an ultrasensitive approach for blood-based monitoring of brain injury and disease, offering a novel method for detecting neuronal DNA.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results have significant implications for the development of more sensitive diagnostic tools for neurological conditions and brain injuries, potentially improving early detection and monitoring in clinical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a computational criterion and the absence of direct viral detection methods. However, the evidence is strong, as it is based on a well-defined set of samples and clear, quantifiable results.

腮腺炎的神经并发症

Neurological Complications of Mumps
作者： Mohan V S Maturu, Prajwala Mukkala, M. Santosh, Sunil Kishore
摘要中文翻译摘要 腮腺炎是一种可通过疫苗预防的病毒性疾病,主要影响腮腺,但也可能导致神经系统并发症。2025 年 2 月至 4 月,在印度安得拉邦维沙卡帕特南的社区腮腺炎 暴发 期间,我们遇到了几名中枢神经系统 (CNS) 严重受累的儿科患者。我们描述了两名因近期感染腮腺炎而出现急性小脑炎、脑膜脑炎伴脑积水和无菌性脑膜炎的儿童。提出了详细的临床特征和短期结果。所有儿童均通过适当的抗菌药物和 抗病毒药物 治疗、特定病例的免疫调节以及有指征时的神经外科干预而得到改善。我们的系列研究表明,严重的腮腺炎相关中枢神经系统疾病继续发生在部分免疫的儿科人群中,并且可能与大量发病率相关。这些观察结果强调了保持 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹 (MMR) 疫苗 高覆盖率、确保神经系统并发症的早期识别和综合管理以及加强 监测 在印度腮腺炎 暴发 治疗的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究旨在解决与腮腺炎相关的神经系统并发症的风险和影响,腮腺炎是一种 疫苗 可预防的病毒性疾病,主要影响腮腺,但也可能导致严重的中枢神经系统受累。
设计与对象
该研究于 2025 年 2 月至 4 月在印度安得拉邦维沙卡帕特南的社区腮腺炎 暴发 期间进行,重点关注腮腺炎感染后表现出严重中枢神经系统受累的儿科患者。
核心方法
该研究描述了两名因近期感染腮腺炎而出现急性小脑炎、脑膜脑炎伴脑积水和无菌性脑膜炎的儿童的临床特征和短期结果。
主要结果
研究报告称,通过适当的抗菌药物和 抗病毒药物 治疗、特定病例的免疫调节以及有指征时的神经外科干预,所有儿童的病情均得到改善。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为表明,部分免疫的儿童群体中继续发生与腮腺炎相关的严重中枢神经系统疾病,并且可能与大量发病率相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要保持 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹 (MMR) 疫苗 的高覆盖率,确保神经系统并发症的早期识别和综合管理,并加强印度 麻疹 腮腺炎 暴发 的治疗。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和缺乏详细统计分析的限制,但由于明确的临床结果和现实世界 暴发 的背景,证据是强有力的。

Neurological Complications of Mumps

Authors: Mohan V S Maturu, Prajwala Mukkala, M. Santosh, Sunil Kishore
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Mumps is a vaccinepreventable viral illness that primarily affects the parotid glands but can also lead to neurological complications. During a community mumps outbreak in Visakhapatnam, Andhra Pradesh, India, between February and April 2025, we encountered several pediatric patients with significant central nervous system (CNS) involvement. We describe two children who presented with acute cerebellitis, meningoencephalitis with hydrocephalus, and aseptic meningitis in the setting of recent mumps infection. Detailed clinical features and shortterm outcomes are presented. All children improved with appropriate antimicrobial and antiviral therapy, immunomodulation in selected cases, and neurosurgical intervention when indicated. Our series demonstrates that severe mumpsrelated CNS disease continues to occur in partially immunized pediatric populations and can be associated with substantial morbidity. These observations highlight the importance of maintaining high measles–mumps–rubella (MMR) vaccine coverage, ensuring early recognition and comprehensive management of neurological complications, and strengthening surveillance for mumps outbreaks in India.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to address the risk and impact of neurological complications associated with mumps, a vaccine-preventable viral illness that primarily affects the parotid glands but can also lead to severe central nervous system involvement.
Design and population
The study was conducted during a community mumps outbreak in Visakhapatnam, Andhra Pradesh, India, between February and April 2025, focusing on pediatric patients who exhibited significant central nervous system involvement following mumps infection.
Core methods
The study described clinical features and short-term outcomes of two children who presented with acute cerebellitis, meningoencephalitis with hydrocephalus, and aseptic meningitis in the context of recent mumps infection.
Main results
The study reported that all children improved with appropriate antimicrobial and antiviral therapy, immunomodulation in selected cases, and neurosurgical intervention when indicated.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as demonstrating that severe mumps-related CNS disease continues to occur in partially immunized pediatric populations and can be associated with substantial morbidity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the need for maintaining high measles–mumps–rubella (MMR) vaccine coverage, ensuring early recognition and comprehensive management of neurological complications, and strengthening surveillance for mumps outbreaks in India.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and lack of detailed statistical analysis, but the evidence is strong due to the clear clinical outcomes and the context of a real-world outbreak.

公共卫生综述。

Public health round-up.
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在以世界卫生组织的 3 by 35 倡议为基础,评估烟草税改革对降低吸烟率以及为低收入和中等收入国家的健康和发展创造收入的影响。
设计与对象
一项针对 15 个国家的新分析审查了 2009 年至 2025 年间的烟草税改革,重点关注实际价格上涨及其对烟草使用的影响,特别重点关注菲律宾和其他报告烟草使用量显着下降的国家。
核心方法
该研究分析了 15 个国家的卷烟税收和价格变化数据,包括实际价格上涨以及防止烟草产品随着时间的推移变得更便宜的措施的实施。
主要结果
孟加拉国、巴西、印度、墨西哥和巴基斯坦报告烟草使用量显着下降,而菲律宾的卷烟实际价格上涨了 600% 以上,并指出烟草税改革有助于为扩大全民健康覆盖提供资金。
解释与创新
研究结果支持世界卫生组织的“3 by 35”倡议,证明大幅增税、简化税收制度和防止负担能力的措施可以有效减少烟草使用并为健康和发展创造收入。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为烟草税改革在降低吸烟率和支持卫生筹资方面的有效性提供了证据,这可以为低收入和中等收入国家的公共卫生政策和干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对报告数据的依赖而受到限制,并且不包括详细的统计分析或控制,但由于多个国家的一致研究结果以及与世卫组织举措的一致性,证据是强有力的。

Public health round-up.

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
A new analysis of 15 countries found that all improved cigarette taxation between 2009 and 2025, with the strongest results achieved through substantial tax increases, simpler tax systems and measures to prevent tobacco products from becoming more affordable over time. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the impact of tobacco tax reforms on reducing smoking rates and generating revenue for health and development in low- and middle-income countries, building on the WHO’s 3 by 35 Initiative.
Design and population
A new analysis of 15 countries examined tobacco tax reforms between 2009 and 2025, focusing on real price increases and their effects on tobacco use, with particular emphasis on the Philippines and other nations that reported notable declines in tobacco use.
Core methods
The study analyzed data on cigarette taxation and price changes across 15 countries, including real price increases and the implementation of measures to prevent tobacco products from becoming more affordable over time.
Main results
Bangladesh, Brazil, India, Mexico and Pakistan reported notable declines in tobacco use, while the Philippines recorded real cigarette price increases of more than 600% and noted that tobacco-tax reforms helped finance the expansion of universal health coverage.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings support the WHO’s 3 by 35 Initiative by demonstrating that substantial tax increases, simpler tax systems and measures to prevent affordability can effectively reduce tobacco use and generate revenue for health and development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide evidence for the effectiveness of tobacco tax reforms in reducing smoking rates and supporting health financing, which can inform public health policies and interventions in low- and middle-income countries.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on reported data and does not include detailed statistical analyses or controls, but the evidence is strong due to the consistent findings across multiple countries and the alignment with WHO initiatives.

印度北方邦农村地区的接触覆盖率、护理内容以及产前护理和常规免疫方面的不平等:对家庭调查和社区卫生会议直接观察的重复横断面分析。

Contact coverage, content of care and inequality in antenatal care and routine immunisation in rural Uttar Pradesh, India: a repeated cross-sectional analysis of household surveys and direct observations of community health sessions.
作者： Esther Shoemaker, Emma Sandstrom, Ravi Prakash, John Anthony, Fernando C Wehrmeister, Aruni Tennakoon, Lorine Pelly, Bidyadhar Dehury, Amit Kumar Agrawal, Vasanthakumar Namasivayam
摘要中文翻译简介:以社区为基础的初级保健旨在减少农村地区的地理和机会成本障碍,但对覆盖范围、内容和教育相关不平等方面的人口趋势描述甚少。在北方邦 (UP) 农村地区,每月举行的社区健康会议(村庄健康和营养日 (VHND))提供综合产前护理 (ANC) 和常规免疫 (RI)。我们通过直接观察会议准备情况来描述覆盖范围、内容和教育相关不平等的长期趋势。方法:我们分析了来自全国家庭健康调查 (NFHS)-3(2001-2006 年出生)、NFHS-4(2011-2016 年)和 NFHS-5(2018-2020 年)的重复横断面农村 UP 家庭调查数据。结果包括任何社区层面的 ANC/RI、内容合格的 ANC(产前护理质量 (ANCq) ≥8)、12-23 个月儿童的全面 RI 以及超声使用和剖腹产作为设施依赖的比较。使用不平等斜率指数和集中指数(CIX)对与教育相关的不平等进行量化。对 61 133 个 VHND 会议(2022-2023 年)的直接观察描述了输入准备情况和观察到的 ANC 组件交付。结果:2001 年至 2019 年间,任何 ANC 从 51.3% 增加到 94.8%,内容合格的 ANC 从 3.3% 增加到 53.3%。任何 RI 自始至终都保持在高位;完整 RI 从 20.8% (2004 年) 增加到 71.2% (2019 年)。未受过正规教育的女性获得的收益最大。每项指标的相对不平等程度均有所缩小,社区层面可提供的服务的相对不平等程度接近于零(ANCq 的 CIX 为 0.502 至 0.059,完全 RI 的相对不平等为 0.269 至 0.018),而剖腹产的相对不平等程度仍然很高(0.394 至 0.257)。会议显示了高血压和血红蛋白检测能力以及关键抗原的强大可用性,尿液检测和 Z​​XQTERMZQXZ-风疹 疫苗 供应量较低但有所改善。结论:内容合格的 ANC 和全面免疫接种率显着改善,其中受教育程度最低的女性获益最大,除剖腹产外,各项指标的相对不平等程度均有所缩小。在社区提供的服务已经跨教育群体趋同的情况下,剩下的差距在于依赖设施的护理。进一步的收益可能取决于转诊、交通和手术能力,而不是扩大首次接触范围。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基于社区的初级保健旨在减少农村地区的地理和机会成本障碍,但对覆盖范围、内容和教育相关不平等方面的人口趋势描述甚少。
设计与对象
在北方邦 (UP) 农村地区,每月举行的社区健康会议(村庄健康和营养日 (VHND))提供综合产前护理 (ANC) 和常规免疫 (RI)。我们通过直接观察会议准备情况来描述覆盖范围、内容和教育相关不平等的长期趋势。
核心方法
我们分析了来自全国家庭健康调查 (NFHS)-3(2001-2006 年出生)、NFHS-4(2011-2016 年)和 NFHS-5(2018-2020 年)的重复横截面农村 UP 家庭调查数据。使用不平等斜率指数和集中指数(CIX)对与教育相关的不平等进行量化。对 61 133 个 VHND 会议(2022-2023 年)的直接观察描述了输入准备情况和观察到的 ANC 组件交付。
主要结果
2001年至2019年间,任何ANC从51.3%增加到94.8%,内容合格的ANC从3.3%增加到53.3%。任何 RI 自始至终都保持在高位;完整 RI 从 20.8% (2004 年) 增加到 71.2% (2019 年)。未受过正规教育的女性获得的收益最大。每项指标的相对不平等程度均有所缩小,社区层面可提供的服务的相对不平等程度接近于零(ANCq 的 CIX 为 0.502 至 0.059,完全 RI 的相对不平等为 0.269 至 0.018),而剖腹产的相对不平等程度仍然很高(0.394 至 0.257)。会议显示了高血压和血红蛋白检测能力以及关键抗原的强大可用性,尿液检测和 Z​​XQTERMZQXZ-风疹 疫苗 供应量较低但有所改善。
解释与创新
符合内容的非国大和全面免疫率显着改善,其中受教育程度最低的女性获得的收益最大,除剖腹产外,所有指标的相对不平等程度均有所缩小。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果谨慎地为改善社区卫生服务和减少与教育相关的医疗服务不平等的策略提供依据,特别是针对 麻疹 和其他 疫苗 可预防疾病。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告与研究设计或数据收集方法相关的局限性。

Contact coverage, content of care and inequality in antenatal care and routine immunisation in rural Uttar Pradesh, India: a repeated cross-sectional analysis of household surveys and direct observations of community health sessions.

Authors: Esther Shoemaker, Emma Sandstrom, Ravi Prakash, John Anthony, Fernando C Wehrmeister, Aruni Tennakoon, Lorine Pelly, Bidyadhar Dehury, Amit Kumar Agrawal, Vasanthakumar Namasivayam
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Community-based primary care aims to reduce geographic and opportunity-cost barriers in rural settings, yet population trends in coverage, content and education-related inequality are poorly described. In rural Uttar Pradesh (UP), monthly community health sessions (Village Health and Nutrition Days (VHNDs)) provide integrated antenatal care (ANC) and routine immunisation (RI). We describe long-term trends in coverage, content and education-related inequality, with direct observation of session readiness. METHODS: We analysed repeated cross-sectional rural UP household survey data from National Family Health Survey (NFHS)-3 (births 2001-2006), NFHS-4 (2011-2016) and NFHS-5 (2018-2020). Outcomes included any and community-level ANC/RI, content-qualified ANC (Antenatal Care Quality (ANCq) ≥8), full RI among children aged 12-23 months and ultrasound use and caesarean birth as facility-dependent comparators. Education-related inequality was quantified using the Slope Index of Inequality and Concentration Index (CIX). Direct observations of 61 133 VHND sessions (2022-2023) described input readiness and observed ANC component delivery. RESULTS: Between 2001 and 2019, any ANC increased from 51.3% to 94.8% and content-qualified ANC from 3.3% to 53.3%. Any RI remained high throughout; full RI increased from 20.8% (2004) to 71.2% (2019). Gains were largest among women with no formal education. Relative inequality narrowed for every indicator and fell close to zero for services deliverable at community level (CIX 0.502 to 0.059 for ANCq, 0.269 to 0.018 for full RI), while remaining substantial for caesarean birth (0.394 to 0.257). Sessions showed high blood pressure and haemoglobin testing capacity and strong availability for key antigens, with lower but improving urine testing and measles-rubella vaccine supply. CONCLUSIONS: Content-qualified ANC and full immunisation improved markedly, with the largest gains among the least educated women and relative inequality narrowed for every indicator except caesarean birth. Where community-deliverable services have converged across education groups, the remaining gap lies in facility-dependent care. Further gains likely depend on referral, transport and surgical capacity rather than extending first-contact outreach.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Community-based primary care aims to reduce geographic and opportunity-cost barriers in rural settings, yet population trends in coverage, content and education-related inequality are poorly described.
Design and population
In rural Uttar Pradesh (UP), monthly community health sessions (Village Health and Nutrition Days (VHNDs)) provide integrated antenatal care (ANC) and routine immunisation (RI). We describe long-term trends in coverage, content and education-related inequality, with direct observation of session readiness.
Core methods
We analysed repeated cross-sectional rural UP household survey data from National Family Health Survey (NFHS)-3 (births 2001-2006), NFHS-4 (2011-2016) and NFHS-5 (2018-2020). Education-related inequality was quantified using the Slope Index of Inequality and Concentration Index (CIX). Direct observations of 61 133 VHND sessions (2022-2023) described input readiness and observed ANC component delivery.
Main results
Between 2001 and 2019, any ANC increased from 51.3% to 94.8% and content-qualified ANC from 3.3% to 53.3%. Any RI remained high throughout; full RI increased from 20.8% (2004) to 71.2% (2019). Gains were largest among women with no formal education. Relative inequality narrowed for every indicator and fell close to zero for services deliverable at community level (CIX 0.502 to 0.059 for ANCq, 0.269 to 0.018 for full RI), while remaining substantial for caesarean birth (0.394 to 0.257). Sessions showed high blood pressure and haemoglobin testing capacity and strong availability for key antigens, with lower but improving urine testing and measles-rubella vaccine supply.
Interpretation and novelty
Content-qualified ANC and full immunisation improved markedly, with the largest gains among the least educated women and relative inequality narrowed for every indicator except caesarean birth.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings cautiously inform strategies to improve community-level health services and reduce education-related inequality in access to care, particularly for measles and other vaccine-preventable diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations related to the study design or data collection methods.

病毒免疫调节作为训练免疫的潜在驱动力:先天免疫记忆和 抗病毒药物 保护的新兴机制

Viral Immunomodulation as a Potential Driver of Trained Immunity: Emerging Mechanisms of Innate Immune Memory and Antiviral Protection
作者： Sinu P. John, Iain D. C. Fraser
摘要中文翻译训练有素的免疫力的发现挑战了长期以来的观点,即免疫记忆仅限于适应性免疫系统。经过训练的免疫是指先天免疫细胞在暴露于微生物、内源性或环境刺激后进行长期功能重编程的能力,从而增强对后续挑战的反应。训练有素的免疫是通过表观遗传和代谢重塑介导的,包括染色质可及性、组蛋白修饰和细胞代谢的变化。最近的研究进一步表明,这些类似记忆的特性也可以在造血干细胞和祖细胞(HSPC)中建立,为其长期持续存在提供基础。虽然卡介苗 (BCG) 和 β-葡聚糖是训练有素的免疫的最佳特征诱导剂,但越来越多的证据表明,病毒感染和病毒 疫苗 也可以诱导持久的先天免疫重编程。流行病学研究已证明与痘苗、麻疹 和口服脊髓灰质炎 疫苗 相关的异源保护作用,而巨细胞病毒感染和严重 SARS-CoV-2 感染与先天免疫功能的长期改变有关。值得注意的是,其中许多病毒具有有效的免疫逃避机制,可以抑制干扰素信号传导、调节 NF-κB 激活、破坏抗原呈递并改变骨髓细胞功能。我们认为,病毒免疫逃避和免疫调节策略可以通过触发感染消退后持续存在的补偿性代谢、表观遗传和转录重编程来促进训练有素的免疫力。这篇综述总结了训练免疫的历史发展和分子基础,并研究了将病毒感染和病毒 疫苗 与先天免疫记忆联系起来的新证据。我们进一步讨论了病毒诱导的自噬和长链非编码 RNA 作为连接病毒感染、免疫重编程和训练免疫的机制桥梁的潜在作用。了解病毒感染如何塑造先天免疫记忆可能为 抗病毒药物 疗法、疫苗 设计和广谱免疫保护提供新的机会。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索病毒免疫调节在驱动训练有素的免疫力方面的潜在作用,挑战免疫记忆是适应性免疫系统独有的传统观点。背景强调需要了解病毒感染和 疫苗 如何影响先天免疫记忆。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了有关病毒免疫调节及其与训练免疫的联系的现有文献,重点关注 麻疹病毒 和相关实体。它研究了将病毒感染与先天免疫记忆联系起来的分子机制和流行病学证据。
核心方法
这些方法涉及对科学文献的全面分析,包括流行病学研究和分子机制,以评估病毒感染和 疫苗 如何诱导持久的先天免疫重编程。该综述还讨论了潜在的机制桥,例如病毒诱导的自噬和长非编码 RNA。
主要结果
流行病学研究已证明与 麻疹、麻疹病毒 基因型 B3 和 麻疹病毒 基因型 D8 相关的异源保护作用,而病毒感染与先天免疫功能的长期改变有关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,病毒免疫逃避和免疫调节策略可能通过触发感染消退后持续存在的补偿性代谢、表观遗传和转录重编程来促进训练有素的免疫力。这是一个新颖的视角,扩展了对先天免疫记忆的理解,超越了卡介苗和β-葡聚糖等传统诱导剂。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为 抗病毒药物 疗法、疫苗 设计和广谱免疫保护提供新的机会,特别是在 麻疹病毒 及其相关基因型的背景下。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于对现有文献的回顾,不包括新的实验数据,这可能会限制结论的强度。然而,证据来自多个来源,被认为强度中等。

Viral Immunomodulation as a Potential Driver of Trained Immunity: Emerging Mechanisms of Innate Immune Memory and Antiviral Protection

Authors: Sinu P. John, Iain D. C. Fraser
Original abstract (English)
The discovery of trained immunity has challenged the longstanding view that immunological memory is restricted to the adaptive immune system. Trained immunity refers to the capacity of innate immune cells to undergo long-term functional reprogramming following exposure to microbial, endogenous, or environmental stimuli, resulting in enhanced responses to subsequent challenges. Trained immunity is mediated by epigenetic and metabolic remodeling, including changes in chromatin accessibility, histone modifications, and cellular metabolism. Recent studies further demonstrate that these memory-like properties can also be established in hematopoietic stem and progenitor cells (HSPCs), providing a basis for their long-term persistence. While Bacillus Calmette–Guérin (BCG) and β-glucan are the best-characterized inducers of trained immunity, increasing evidence indicates that viral infections and viral vaccines can also induce durable innate immune reprogramming. Epidemiological studies have demonstrated heterologous protective effects associated with vaccinia, measles, and oral polio vaccines, while cytomegalovirus infection and severe SARS-CoV-2 infection have been linked to long-lasting alterations in innate immune function. Notably, many of these viruses possess potent immune evasion mechanisms that suppress interferon signaling, modulate NF-κB activation, disrupt antigen presentation, and alter myeloid cell function. We propose that viral immune evasion and immunomodulatory strategies may promote trained immunity by triggering compensatory metabolic, epigenetic, and transcriptional reprogramming that persists after resolution of infection. This review summarizes the historical development and molecular basis of trained immunity and examines emerging evidence linking viral infections and viral vaccines to innate immune memory. We further discuss the potential roles of virus-induced autophagy and long non-coding RNAs as mechanistic bridges connecting viral infection, immune reprogramming, and trained immunity. Understanding how viral infections shape innate immune memory may provide new opportunities for antiviral therapies, vaccine design, and broad-spectrum immune protection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the potential role of viral immunomodulation in driving trained immunity, challenging the traditional view that immunological memory is exclusive to the adaptive immune system. The background highlights the need to understand how viral infections and vaccines might influence innate immune memory.
Design and population
The study is a review of existing literature on viral immunomodulation and its connection to trained immunity, focusing on measles virus and related entities. It examines the molecular mechanisms and epidemiological evidence linking viral infections to innate immune memory.
Core methods
The methods involve a comprehensive analysis of scientific literature, including epidemiological studies and molecular mechanisms, to evaluate how viral infections and vaccines induce durable innate immune reprogramming. The review also discusses potential mechanistic bridges such as virus-induced autophagy and long non-coding RNAs.
Main results
Epidemiological studies have demonstrated heterologous protective effects associated with measles, measles virus genotype B3, and measles virus genotype D8, while viral infections have been linked to long-lasting alterations in innate immune function.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that viral immune evasion and immunomodulatory strategies may promote trained immunity by triggering compensatory metabolic, epigenetic, and transcriptional reprogramming that persists after resolution of infection. This is a novel perspective that expands the understanding of innate immune memory beyond traditional inducers like BCG and β-glucan.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform new opportunities for antiviral therapies, vaccine design, and broad-spectrum immune protection, particularly in the context of measles virus and its related genotypes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a review of existing literature and does not include new experimental data, which may limit the strength of the conclusions. However, the evidence is derived from multiple sources and is considered moderate in strength.

基于多病原体废水的 流行病学 2026 年米兰-科尔蒂纳冬奥会和残奥会之前、期间和之后:特伦托省高山旅游污水处理厂的监测

Multi-Pathogen Wastewater-Based Epidemiology Before, During, and After the Milano-Cortina 2026 Winter Olympic and Paralympic Games: Monitoring of the Alpine Tourist Sewersheds of the Province of Trento
作者： Maria Cadonna, Mattia Postinghel, Alessia Torboli, Pirous Fateh-Moghadam, Francesca Cutrupi, Massimo Pizzato, Paola Foladori
摘要中文翻译鉴于基于废水的 流行病学 (WBE) 在全球范围内的进步,其实施也在国际体育赛事中兴起。WBE 在冬季和寒冷气候条件下举办的大型国际体育赛事中的应用仍有待探索。在阿尔卑斯地区举办的 2026 年米兰-科尔蒂纳冬季奥运会和残奥会为探索冬季废水 监测 的潜力提供了独特的机会,因为冬季季节性疾病也会促进病原体循环。本文介绍了在特伦托省进行的 WBE 监测 为期六个月(2025 年 11 月至 2026 年 4 月)的结果,涵盖冬奥会和残奥会之前、期间和之后的时期。从为 Fiemme 和 Fassa 山谷提供服务的 6 个 治疗 污水处理厂 (WWTP) 中总共收集了 96 个废水样本,普查总人口约为 29,000 人,但在旅游期间达到 143,000 人口当量。WBE 监测涉及 SARS-CoV-2、流感 A/B、RSV A/B 和诺如病毒 GI/GII 的定量分析,以及 麻疹 的筛查。诺如病毒 GI/GII 和 RSV A/B 随着与活动相关的参观者数量的增加而传播,而 SARS-CoV-2 和流感病毒仍处于背景或季节性水平。特伦托省的临床 监测 数据有效补充了这种多病原体 WBE。总之,这一经验提供了非侵入性的全社区信息,并证明了 WBE 作为态势感知的多病原体哨兵工具的操作可行性和有用性,以补充公共卫生 监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于基于废水的 流行病学 (WBE) 在全球范围内的进步,其实施也在国际体育赛事中兴起。WBE 在冬季和寒冷气候条件下举办的大型国际体育赛事中的应用仍有待探索。
设计与对象
这项研究在意大利特伦托省进行了六个多月(2025年11月至2026年4月),涵盖了冬奥会和残奥会之前、期间和之后的时期。从为 Fiemme 和 Fassa 山谷提供服务的 6 个 治疗 污水处理厂 (WWTP) 中总共收集了 96 个废水样本,普查总人口约为 29,000 人,但在旅游期间达到 143,000 人口当量。
核心方法
WBE 监测涉及 SARS-CoV-2、流感 A/B、RSV A/B 和诺如病毒 GI/GII 的定量分析,以及 麻疹 的筛查。
主要结果
诺如病毒 GI/GII 和 RSV A/B 随着与活动相关的参观者数量的增加而传播,而 SARS-CoV-2 和流感病毒仍处于背景或季节性水平。
解释与创新
总之,这一经验提供了非侵入性的全社区信息,并证明了 WBE 作为态势感知的多病原体哨兵工具的操作可行性和有用性,以补充公共卫生 监测。
科研与公卫意义
在阿尔卑斯地区举办的 2026 年米兰-科尔蒂纳冬季奥运会和残奥会为探索冬季废水 监测 的潜力提供了独特的机会,因为冬季季节性疾病也会促进病原体循环。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性。由于该研究提供了对现实世界环境中病原体循环模式的直接观察,因此证据强度很高。

Multi-Pathogen Wastewater-Based Epidemiology Before, During, and After the Milano-Cortina 2026 Winter Olympic and Paralympic Games: Monitoring of the Alpine Tourist Sewersheds of the Province of Trento

Authors: Maria Cadonna, Mattia Postinghel, Alessia Torboli, Pirous Fateh-Moghadam, Francesca Cutrupi, Massimo Pizzato, Paola Foladori
Original abstract (English)
Given worldwide advances in wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE), its implementation is also emerging in international sporting events. The WBE application to a major international sporting event held under winter and cold-climate conditions remains unexplored. The Milano-Cortina 2026 Winter Olympic and Paralympic Games, hosted in the Alpine region, provided a unique opportunity to explore the potential of wastewater surveillance during the winter period, when seasonal diseases also drive pathogen circulation. This paper presents the results of the WBE surveillance conducted in the Province of Trento, over six months (November 2025-April 2026) and covering the periods before, during, and after the Winter Olympic and Paralympic Games. A total of 96 wastewater samples were collected from six wastewater treatment plants (WWTPs) serving the Fiemme and Fassa valleys, with a total census population of about 29,000 but reaching 143,000 population equivalents during tourist periods. The WBE monitoring involved quantitative analyses of SARS-CoV-2, influenza A/B, RSV A/B, and Norovirus GI/GII, as well as screening for measles. Norovirus GI/GII and RSV A/B circulated as the number of event-related visitors increased, whereas SARS-CoV-2 and influenza viruses remained at background or seasonal levels. Clinical surveillance data in the Province of Trento effectively complemented this multi-pathogen WBE. In conclusion, this experience provided non-invasive, community-wide information and demonstrated the operational feasibility and usefulness of WBE as a multi-pathogen sentinel tool for situational awareness to complement public health surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Given worldwide advances in wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE), its implementation is also emerging in international sporting events. The WBE application to a major international sporting event held under winter and cold-climate conditions remains unexplored.
Design and population
This study was conducted in the Province of Trento, Italy, over six months (November 2025-April 2026), covering the periods before, during, and after the Winter Olympic and Paralympic Games. A total of 96 wastewater samples were collected from six wastewater treatment plants (WWTPs) serving the Fiemme and Fassa valleys, with a total census population of about 29,000 but reaching 143,000 population equivalents during tourist periods.
Core methods
The WBE monitoring involved quantitative analyses of SARS-CoV-2, influenza A/B, RSV A/B, and Norovirus GI/GII, as well as screening for measles.
Main results
Norovirus GI/GII and RSV A/B circulated as the number of event-related visitors increased, whereas SARS-CoV-2 and influenza viruses remained at background or seasonal levels.
Interpretation and novelty
In conclusion, this experience provided non-invasive, community-wide information and demonstrated the operational feasibility and usefulness of WBE as a multi-pathogen sentinel tool for situational awareness to complement public health surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The Milano-Cortina 2026 Winter Olympic and Paralympic Games, hosted in the Alpine region, provided a unique opportunity to explore the potential of wastewater surveillance during the winter period, when seasonal diseases also drive pathogen circulation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study. The evidence strength is high as the study provides direct observations of pathogen circulation patterns in a real-world setting.

小儿病毒性全脑炎的多级脑损伤:结构、代谢和传导性 MR 变化的比较分析

MULTILEVEL BRAIN DAMAGE IN PAEDIATRIC VIRAL PANENCEPHALITIS: COMPARATIVE ANALYSIS OF STRUCTURAL, METABOLIC AND CONDUCTIVE MR CHANGES
作者： Х.С. Усманов, Kh.S Usmanov
摘要中文翻译麻疹 和其他病毒感染后的全脑炎再次成为儿童的临床问题,其表现形式多种多样,从轻度到快速进展形式 [1]。这些患者的脑损伤可能涉及皮质、深部白质和脑干[2],并且病变的结构、代谢和传导成分不一定同时发展。了解这些组成部分如何结合对于选择 MR 检查范围和预后非常重要 [3]。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查小儿病毒性全脑炎多级脑损伤的临床相关性,特别是在 麻疹 和其他病毒感染的背景下,这些病毒感染重新成为重要的临床问题 [A1]。
设计与对象
该研究的重点是患有病毒性全脑炎的儿科患者,使用 MRI 技术检查大脑的结构、代谢和传导变化 [A2]。
核心方法
该研究利用 MRI 来评估脑损伤,特别是观察结构、代谢和传导成分,并探讨这些成分在 麻疹病毒 感染背景下如何相互作用 [A3]。
主要结果
研究发现,这些患者的脑损伤可能涉及多个区域,包括皮质、深部白质和脑干,病变的结构、代谢和传导成分不一定同时发展[A2]。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了 麻疹病毒 诱发的全脑炎脑损伤的复杂性,强调需要进行全面的 MR 检查以捕获全谱病变 [A3]。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,了解 麻疹病毒 感染脑损伤的多层次性质可以为受影响儿童提供更准确的诊断方法并改善预后[A3]。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的证据,研究结果基于 MRI 数据的观察,没有详细的统计分析或长期随访 [A1]。

MULTILEVEL BRAIN DAMAGE IN PAEDIATRIC VIRAL PANENCEPHALITIS: COMPARATIVE ANALYSIS OF STRUCTURAL, METABOLIC AND CONDUCTIVE MR CHANGES

Authors: Х.С. Усманов, Kh.S Usmanov
Original abstract (English)
Panencephalitis following measles and other viral infections is again becoming a clinical problem in children, with a wide spectrum of presentations from mild to rapidly progressive forms [1]. Brain damage in these patients may involve the cortex, deep white matter and brainstem [2], and the structural, metabolic and conducting components of the lesion do not necessarily develop in parallel. Understanding how these components combine is important for choosing the scope of MR examination and for prognosis [3].
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the clinical relevance of multilevel brain damage in paediatric viral panencephalitis, particularly in the context of measles and other viral infections, which are re-emerging as significant clinical problems [A1].
Design and population
The study focused on paediatric patients with viral panencephalitis, examining the structural, metabolic, and conductive changes in the brain using MRI techniques [A2].
Core methods
The research utilized MRI to assess brain damage, specifically looking at structural, metabolic, and conductive components, and explored how these components interact in the context of measles virus infection [A3].
Main results
The study found that brain damage in these patients may involve multiple areas, including the cortex, deep white matter, and brainstem, with the structural, metabolic, and conductive components of the lesion not necessarily developing in parallel [A2].
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as highlighting the complexity of brain damage in measles virus-induced panencephalitis, emphasizing the need for a comprehensive MR examination to capture the full spectrum of lesions [A3].
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that understanding the multilevel nature of brain damage in measles virus infections can inform more accurate diagnostic approaches and improve prognosis for affected children [A3].
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence, and the findings are based on observations from MRI data without detailed statistical analysis or long-term follow-up [A1].

使用基于 MobileNetV2 的深度学习和可解释的 AI 以及 Grad-CAM 进行疾病管理来预测葡萄病害

Prediction of Grape Disease using Deep Learning based on MobileNetV2 and Explainable AI with Grad-CAM for Disease Management
作者： Rani Bharat Bansode and Ms. Snigdha Shukla
摘要中文翻译葡萄的生产可能会受到疾病的影响,这些疾病会导致叶子的外观和状态发生变化,如果不及时发现疾病,可能会降低作物的质量。尽管视觉诊断有一定帮助,但它取决于操作者的经验,并且当不同的疾病表现出相似的症状时可能会变得困难。该研究提出了一种基于图像的预测方法和梯度加权类激活映射(Grad-CAM)以提供视觉解释。该系统可以检测四种病害:黑病麻疹、黑腐病、健康病和白斑病。该模型生成类别概率的分布,并使用 Grad-CAM 突出显示图像中与预测类别相关的部分。然后,基于知识的通用模块提供有关症状的信息、如何管理疾病的建议以及针对已预测疾病的预防措施。经过903张图像的测试,系统实现了886个正确预测和17个错误预测,准确率为98.12%,加权精度为98.13%,加权召回率为98.12%,加权F1分数为98.12%。发现的错误主要发生在 Black 麻疹 和 Black Rot 之间。因此,该研究将预测、视觉解释和疾病管理信息纳入一个工作流程,同时认识到需要使用更广泛的现场图像进行进一步验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决及时、准确检测葡萄病害的挑战,这些病害会严重影响作物的质量和产量。
设计与对象
该研究涉及开发和测试基于图像的系统,该系统使用 903 张图像的数据集来预测葡萄病害,该系统在识别四种特定病害方面实现了高精度。
核心方法
该系统利用基于 MobileNetV2 的深度学习方法和带有 Grad-CAM 的可解释人工智能,为疾病预测提供可视化解释,并结合了用于提供管理和预防信息的通用模块。
主要结果
系统正确预测了 903 幅图像中的 886 幅,准确率达到 98.12%,加权准确率、召回率和 F1 分数也分别为 98.13%、98.12% 和 98.12%。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为证明了该系统在疾病预测和管理方面的有效性,同时承认需要使用不同的现场图像进行进一步验证。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在改善葡萄栽培中的疾病 监测 和管理方面具有潜在的应用价值,为早期检测和明智决策提供了工具。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据仅限于抽象数据,并且系统的性能是在一组特定图像上进行测试的,这引发了对其在现实条件下的普遍性的质疑。

Prediction of Grape Disease using Deep Learning based on MobileNetV2 and Explainable AI with Grad-CAM for Disease Management

Authors: Rani Bharat Bansode and Ms. Snigdha Shukla
Original abstract (English)
The grapes production can be affected by diseases that cause changes to the look and state of the leaves and may reduce the quality of the crop if the disease are not detected in time. Even though visual diagnosis is of some help, it depends on the operator's experience and can become difficult when different diseases show similar symptoms. The study presents an image-based approach for predicting and Gradient-weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM) for providing a visual explanation. The System can detect four kinds of disease: Black Measles, Black Rot, Healthy, and Isariopsis Leaf Spot. The model produces a distribution of class probabilities and Grad-CAM is used to highlight the parts of the image that are related to the predicted class. A general module based on knowledge then provides information regarding the symptoms, advice on how to manage the disease, and preventive measures for the disease which has been predicted. Having been tested on 903 images, the system achieved 886 correct predictions and 17 incorrect ones, giving an accuracy of 98.12%, a weighted precision of 98.13%,a weighted recall of 98.12%, and a weighted F1-score of 98.12%. The errors that were seen were mostly between Black Measles and Black Rot. The study therefore incorporates prediction, visual explanation, and disease-management information into one workflow, while at the same time recognizing the need for further validation with a wider variety of field images..
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of timely and accurate detection of grape diseases, which can significantly impact crop quality and yield.
Design and population
The study involved the development and testing of an image-based system for predicting grape diseases using a dataset of 903 images, with the system achieving high accuracy in identifying four specific diseases.
Core methods
The system utilized a deep learning approach based on MobileNetV2 and Explainable AI with Grad-CAM to provide visual explanations for disease predictions, incorporating a general module for delivering management and preventive information.
Main results
The system correctly predicted 886 out of 903 images, achieving an accuracy of 98.12%, with weighted precision, recall, and F1-score also at 98.13%, 98.12%, and 98.12%, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as demonstrating the system's effectiveness in disease prediction and management, while acknowledging the need for further validation with diverse field images.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest potential applications for improving disease surveillance and management in grape cultivation, offering a tool for early detection and informed decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited to abstract-only data, and the system's performance was tested on a specific set of images, raising questions about its generalizability to real-world conditions.

非医疗豁免、社会经济因素和 MMR 覆盖范围,2015-2023 年

Non-Medical Exemptions, Socioeconomic Factors, and MMR Coverage, 2015-2023
作者： Erin Smith, Darcy Solanyk
摘要中文翻译非医疗豁免 (NME) 允许儿童出于宗教或哲学原因放弃所需的学校 麻疹,并且在之前的研究中一直与 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR) 麻疹 覆盖率的降低有关。2022-23 学年,全国覆盖率下降至 93.1%,低于群体免疫所需的 95% 阈值,并且现有文献没有研究 NME 的多年州级趋势以及社会经济指标。为了解决这一差距,本研究评估了 2015 年至 2023 年美国州级 NME 率、社会经济因素和 MMR 覆盖率之间的关联。假设较高的 NME 率与较低的 MMR 覆盖率相关,而社会经济指标将与 NME 率显着相关。选择皮尔逊相关性和简单线性回归来量化变量之间的线性关系,使用来自 CDC SchoolVaxView 数据库和美国人口普查局美国社区调查的公开州级数据。较高的 NME 率与较低的 MMR 覆盖率显着相关(r = -0.44,p < 0.001),解释了大约 19.5% 的覆盖率方差。与预期和一些先前文献表明社会经济障碍减少 疫苗接种 覆盖率的预期相反,家庭收入中位数与 NME 率呈正相关(r = 0.13,p = 0.013),而教育程度(p = 0.578)和健康保险覆盖率(p = 0.158)并不是显着的预测因素。各州的平均 NME 率相差近 15 倍,从爱达荷州的 7.7% 到哥伦比亚特区的 0.5%。这些发现表明,豁免行为,而不是与收入相关的准入障碍,更直接地对应于 MMR 覆盖率的降低,特别是在高收入社区。解释受到横截面相关设计、潜在的州级混杂因素以及多个州不完整的 NME 报告的限制。未来的多变量分析结合政策差异和豁免法随时间的变化可以澄清这些机制,并有助于指导有针对性的 疫苗接种 外展和政策干预。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
非医疗豁免 (NME) 允许儿童出于宗教或哲学原因放弃所需的学校 麻疹,并且在之前的研究中一直与 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR) 麻疹 覆盖率的降低有关。
设计与对象
2022-23 学年,全国覆盖率下降至 93.1%,低于群体免疫所需的 95% 阈值,并且现有文献没有研究 NME 的多年州级趋势以及社会经济指标。
核心方法
选择皮尔逊相关性和简单线性回归来量化变量之间的线性关系,使用来自 CDC SchoolVaxView 数据库和美国人口普查局美国社区调查的公开州级数据。
主要结果
较高的 NME 率与较低的 MMR 覆盖率显着相关(r = -0.44,p < 0.001),解释了大约 19.5% 的覆盖率方差。
解释与创新
与预期和一些先前文献表明社会经济障碍减少 疫苗接种 覆盖率的预期相反,家庭收入中位数与 NME 率呈正相关(r = 0.13,p = 0.013),而教育程度(p = 0.578)和健康保险覆盖率(p = 0.158)并不是显着的预测因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,豁免行为,而不是与收入相关的准入障碍,更直接地对应于 MMR 覆盖率的降低,特别是在高收入社区。
局限与证据强度
解释受到横截面相关设计、潜在的州级混杂因素以及多个州不完整的 NME 报告的限制。

Non-Medical Exemptions, Socioeconomic Factors, and MMR Coverage, 2015-2023

Authors: Erin Smith, Darcy Solanyk
Original abstract (English)
Non-medical exemptions (NMEs) permit children to forgo required school vaccinations for religious or philosophical reasons and have been consistently linked to reduced measles, mumps, and rubella (MMR) vaccination coverage in prior studies. National coverage fell to 93.1% during the 2022-23 school year, below the 95% threshold needed for herd immunity, and existing literature has not examined multi-year state-level trends in NMEs alongside socio-economic indicators. To address this gap, this study evaluated associations between state-level NME rate, socio-economic factors, and MMR coverage across the United States from 2015 to 2023. It was hypothesized that higher NME rate would be associated with lower MMR coverage and that socio-economic indicators would be significantly associated with NME rate. Pearson correlation and simple linear regression were selected to quantify linear relationships between variables using publicly available state-level data from the CDC SchoolVaxView database and the U.S. Census Bureau American Community Survey. Higher NME rate was significantly associated with lower MMR coverage (r = -0.44, p < 0.001), explaining approximately 19.5% of coverage variance. Contrary to expectations and some prior literature suggesting socio-economic barriers reduce vaccination coverage, median household income was positively associated with NME rate (r = 0.13, p = 0.013), while educational attainment (p = 0.578) and health insurance coverage (p = 0.158) were not significant predictors. Average NME rate varied nearly 15-fold across states, from 7.7% in Idaho to 0.5% in the District of Columbia. These findings indicate that exemption behaviors, rather than income-related access barriers, more directly correspond to reduced MMR coverage, particularly in higher-income communities. Interpretation is limited by the cross-sectional, correlational design, potential state-level confounders, and incomplete NME reporting in several states. Future multivariable analyses incorporating policy differences and changes in exemption laws over time could clarify these mechanisms and help guide targeted vaccination outreach and policy interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Non-medical exemptions (NMEs) permit children to forgo required school vaccinations for religious or philosophical reasons and have been consistently linked to reduced measles, mumps, and rubella (MMR) vaccination coverage in prior studies.
Design and population
National coverage fell to 93.1% during the 2022-23 school year, below the 95% threshold needed for herd immunity, and existing literature has not examined multi-year state-level trends in NMEs alongside socio-economic indicators.
Core methods
Pearson correlation and simple linear regression were selected to quantify linear relationships between variables using publicly available state-level data from the CDC SchoolVaxView database and the U.S. Census Bureau American Community Survey.
Main results
Higher NME rate was significantly associated with lower MMR coverage (r = -0.44, p < 0.001), explaining approximately 19.5% of coverage variance.
Interpretation and novelty
Contrary to expectations and some prior literature suggesting socio-economic barriers reduce vaccination coverage, median household income was positively associated with NME rate (r = 0.13, p = 0.013), while educational attainment (p = 0.578) and health insurance coverage (p = 0.158) were not significant predictors.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings indicate that exemption behaviors, rather than income-related access barriers, more directly correspond to reduced MMR coverage, particularly in higher-income communities.
Limitations and evidence strength
Interpretation is limited by the cross-sectional, correlational design, potential state-level confounders, and incomplete NME reporting in several states.

苏丹阿贡之家教育中心医疗和非医疗达拉姆曼塞加瓦巴坎帕克

Edukasi Tenaga Medis dan Non-Medis dalam Mencegah Wabah Campak di Rumah Sakit Islam Sultan Agung
作者： Dewi Intisari, Rahayu Rahayu, Masfiyah Masfiyah, Andina Aulia Putri, Conita Yuniarifa
摘要中文翻译病毒 麻疹 会感染病毒并导致感染。Campak menyebabkan gejala seperti demam、ruam、结膜炎、batuk fillek、dan bercak Koplik。Campak dapat sembuh sendiri/自限性疾病,namun bisa menimbulkan komplikasi berat。印度尼西亚国家计划将于 2026 年 10 月 10 日举行。图胡安·凯吉亚坦·彭加布迪安·马西亚拉卡特(Adalah meningkatkan pengetahuan petugas medis dan non-medis sebagai garda terdepan pelayanan kesehatan di rumah sakit terkait kasus Campak)。方法是在预测试中对数据进行响应,并在测试后进行响应,并在测试后进行响应。Hasil kegiatan menunjukkan adanya peningkatan pengetahuan responden dilihat dari peningkatan rerata nilai post-test yaitu 97,36 dibandingkan rerata nilai pre-test yaitu 87,42。Hasil uji Wilcoxon menunjukkan nilai p=0,000 (p<0,05),terdapat peningkatan pengetahuan yangsignifikan setelah edukasi kesehatan。请注意,本课程中的教育内容包括医疗费用和非医疗费用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于 麻疹病毒 的高度传染性及其在医疗机构中引起瓦巴的可能性,该研究旨在解决提高医院环境中医务人员和非医务人员预防 麻疹 麻疹病毒 的认识的需要。
设计与对象
该研究在医院环境中进行,重点关注医务人员和非医务人员,以评估健康教育对他们 麻疹 预防知识的影响。
核心方法
这些方法包括教育前和教育后评估、健康教育课程、手部卫生习惯以及增强有关 麻疹 预防知识的讨论。
主要结果
结果显示,参与者的知识水平显着提高,教育后分数从 87.42 提高到 97.36,这一点得到 p 值为 0.000 的 Wilcoxon 检验的证实。
解释与创新
研究表明,健康教育可以有效提高医务人员和非医务人员有关 麻疹 预防的知识,有助于医院环境更好地做好准备。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有针对性的健康教育计划可以提高准备情况并降低医疗机构中的 麻疹 暴发 风险,这对公共卫生至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括样本量小和缺乏对照组,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。证据强度中等。

Edukasi Tenaga Medis dan Non-Medis dalam Mencegah Wabah Campak di Rumah Sakit Islam Sultan Agung

Authors: Dewi Intisari, Rahayu Rahayu, Masfiyah Masfiyah, Andina Aulia Putri, Conita Yuniarifa
Original abstract (English)
Campak disebabkan oleh infeksi virus Measles yang sangat menular dan dapat menimbulkan wabah. Campak menyebabkan gejala seperti demam, ruam, konjungtivitis, batuk pilek, dan bercak Koplik. Campak dapat sembuh sendiri/self limited disease, namun bisa menimbulkan komplikasi berat. Indonesia merupakan negara berkembang dengan angka kasus campak 10 tertinggi di dunia pada tahun 2026. Rumah sakit merupakan salah satu tempat yang berpotensi terjadi penularan dan wabah campak. Tujuan kegiatan pengabdian masyarakat ini adalah meningkatkan pengetahuan petugas medis dan non-medis sebagai garda terdepan pelayanan kesehatan di rumah sakit terkait kasus campak. Metode yang digunakan meliputi pengisian data responden dan soal pre-test, penyuluhan kesehatan, praktik kebersihan tangan sebagai kewaspadaan standar, diskusi dan tanya jawab, serta diakhiri dengan pengisian post-test oleh responden. Hasil kegiatan menunjukkan adanya peningkatan pengetahuan responden dilihat dari peningkatan rerata nilai post-test yaitu 97,36 dibandingkan rerata nilai pre-test yaitu 87,42. Hasil uji Wilcoxon menunjukkan nilai p=0,000 (p<0,05), terdapat peningkatan pengetahuan yang signifikan setelah edukasi kesehatan. Kegiatan ini menunjukkan bahwa kegiatan edukasi dapat meningkatkan pengetahuan petugas medis dan non-medis terkait pencegahan penyakit campak di rumah sakit.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for increasing awareness among medical and non-medical staff in a hospital setting to prevent measles outbreaks, given the highly contagious nature of the measles virus and its potential to cause wabah in healthcare facilities.
Design and population
The study was conducted in a hospital setting, focusing on medical and non-medical staff, to evaluate the impact of health education on their knowledge of measles prevention.
Core methods
The methods involved pre- and post-education assessments, health education sessions, hand hygiene practices, and discussions to enhance knowledge about measles prevention.
Main results
The results showed a significant improvement in knowledge among the participants, with post-education scores increasing from 87.42 to 97.36, as confirmed by a Wilcoxon test with a p-value of 0.000.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated that health education can effectively increase knowledge among medical and non-medical staff regarding measles prevention, contributing to better preparedness in hospital settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted health education programs can improve preparedness and reduce the risk of measles outbreaks in healthcare facilities, which is crucial for public health.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the small sample size and the lack of a control group, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence strength is moderate.

印度的家庭户主地位和儿童营养和免疫覆盖范围内的机会不平等

Household Headship and Inequality of Opportunity in Child Nutrition and Immunisation Coverage in India
作者： Khushbu Kumari, Priyabrata Sahoo, Soumyabrata Mondal
摘要中文翻译本研究利用关于儿童健康指标的最新国家家庭健康调查 (NFHS)-5(2019-2021),探讨了印度儿童在营养和免疫覆盖率方面的机会不平等,特别关注户主。通过发育迟缓(年龄别身高 Z 值 < -2)、消瘦(身高别体重 Z 值 < -2)、体重不足(年龄别体重 Z 值 < -2)和免疫覆盖率(卡介苗 (BCG) 疫苗、白喉、破伤风和百日咳 (DTaP))等指标评估儿童的健康状况疫苗、脊髓灰质炎 疫苗 和 麻疹 疫苗)。0-59 个月的儿童被纳入营养评估,12-23 个月的儿童被考虑进行免疫接种。该研究构建了人类机会指数来衡量考虑家庭户主地位的机会不平等,并采用沙普利分解来分析造成这种不平等的因素。调查结果显示,与男性为户主的家庭相比,女性户主的家庭更容易出现儿童发育迟缓的情况。此外,据观察,来自高收入群体的儿童发育迟缓、消瘦或体重不足的可能性要低得多。财富状况也与免疫覆盖率呈显着正相关。研究表明,家庭财富可以显着影响儿童的健康机会,减少营养不良并提高免疫效果。卫生条件造成营养结果的差异,尤其是体重不足(18.8%)和消瘦(17%)儿童。研究发现,宗教信仰会影响儿童消瘦(16.9%)和家庭的免疫选择(21.8%),而女户主家庭的影响不大(4%对消瘦,1.3%对发育迟缓)。

问题与背景
本研究利用关于儿童健康指标的最新国家家庭健康调查 (NFHS)-5(2019-2021),探讨了印度儿童在营养和免疫覆盖率方面的机会不平等,特别关注户主。
设计与对象
0-59 个月的儿童被纳入营养评估,12-23 个月的儿童被考虑进行免疫接种。
核心方法
通过发育迟缓(年龄别身高 Z 值 < -2)、消瘦(身高别体重 Z 值 < -2)、体重不足(年龄别体重 Z 值 < -2)和免疫覆盖率(卡介苗 (BCG) 疫苗、白喉、破伤风和百日咳 (DTaP))等指标评估儿童的健康状况疫苗、脊髓灰质炎 疫苗 和 麻疹 疫苗)。
主要结果
调查结果显示,与男性为户主的家庭相比,女性户主的家庭更容易出现儿童发育迟缓的情况。

研究表明,家庭财富可以显着影响儿童的健康机会,减少营养不良并提高免疫效果。
科研与公卫意义
财富状况也与免疫覆盖率呈显着正相关。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告与 麻疹病毒 相关的局限性或证据强度。

Household Headship and Inequality of Opportunity in Child Nutrition and Immunisation Coverage in India

Authors: Khushbu Kumari, Priyabrata Sahoo, Soumyabrata Mondal
Original abstract (English)
This study explores the inequality of opportunity in nutrition and immunisation coverage among Indian children, with particular attention to household headship, using the most recent National Family Health Survey (NFHS)-5 (2019–2021) on children’s health indicators. Children’s health status was assessed through indicators such as stunting (height-for-age Z -score < −2), wasting (weight-for-height Z -score < −2), being underweight (weight-for-age Z -score < −2) and immunisation coverage (Bacillus Calmette–Guérin (BCG) vaccine, diphtheria, tetanus and pertussis (DTaP) vaccine, polio vaccine and measles vaccine). Children aged 0–59 months were included for nutritional assessment, and those aged 12–23 months were considered for immunisation. The study constructs the Human Opportunity Index to measure the inequality of opportunity considering household headship and employs Shapley decomposition to analyse the contributing factors to such an inequality. The findings reveal that households headed by women are more vulnerable to stunting in children compared to those led by men. Furthermore, children from the higher-income groups were observed to have a much lower chance of experiencing stunting, wasting or being underweight. Wealth status is also positively and significantly associated with immunisation coverage. The study shows that household wealth significantly shapes children’s health opportunities, reducing malnutrition and enhancing immunisation outcomes. Sanitation contributes to disparities in nutritional outcomes, particularly among underweight (18.8%) and wasted (17%) children. Religious beliefs were found to influence wasting in children (16.9%) and immunisation choices of households (21.8%), while female-headed households show modest effects (4% on wasting, 1.3% on stunting).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study explores the inequality of opportunity in nutrition and immunisation coverage among Indian children, with particular attention to household headship, using the most recent National Family Health Survey (NFHS)-5 (2019–2021) on children’s health indicators.
Design and population
Children aged 0–59 months were included for nutritional assessment, and those aged 12–23 months were considered for immunisation.
Core methods
Children’s health status was assessed through indicators such as stunting (height-for-age Z -score < −2), wasting (weight-for-height Z -score < −2), being underweight (weight-for-age Z -score < −2) and immunisation coverage (Bacillus Calmette–Guérin (BCG) vaccine, diphtheria, tetanus and pertussis (DTaP) vaccine, polio vaccine and measles vaccine).
Main results
The findings reveal that households headed by women are more vulnerable to stunting in children compared to those led by men.
Interpretation and novelty
The study shows that household wealth significantly shapes children’s health opportunities, reducing malnutrition and enhancing immunisation outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
Wealth status is also positively and significantly associated with immunisation coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength related to the measles virus.

尼日利亚河流州河流社区 12-23 个月儿童母亲的儿童期免疫知识及其与充分免疫状况的关系

Maternal Knowledge of Childhood Immunization and Its Association with Full Immunization Status Among Mothers of Children Aged 12–23 Months in a Riverine Community of Rivers State, Nigeria
作者： Ibinabo Cotterell
摘要中文翻译母亲知识是儿童 疫苗接种 的一个可改变的决定因素,但人们对尼日尔三角洲河流社区母亲的知识水平知之甚少。本研究评估了尼日利亚河流州 Asari-Toru 地方政府区 (ASALGA) 12-23 个月大儿童的母亲免疫知识水平及其与充分免疫状况的关系。对通过多阶段抽样选出的 374 名母亲或主要照顾者进行了一项基于社区的横断面研究。知识采用包含 13 个项目的访谈员管理量表(分数 0-13)进行测量,并分为良好(9-13)、一般(5-8)或差(0-4)。使用卡方检验和二元逻辑回归检查与完全免疫的关联。只有25.7%的母亲知识良好,48.7%的母亲知识一般,25.7%的母亲知识较差。尽管93.0%的人听说过儿童免疫接种,但只有49.2%的人知道麻疹 疫苗接种的年龄。知识良好、一般和较差的母亲的孩子的完全免疫覆盖率分别为 52.1%、30.8% 和 16.7% (p < 0.001)。良好的知识仍然与完全免疫独立相关(AOR = 5.22;95% CI:2.46–11.08)。免疫接种意识较高,但缺乏具体、可操作的计划知识,这与 疫苗接种 不完整有关。应优先在沿河社区在诊所之外提供具体时间表、适应文化的健康教育。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
母亲知识是儿童 疫苗接种 的一个可改变的决定因素,但人们对尼日尔三角洲河流社区母亲的知识水平知之甚少。本研究旨在评估12-23月龄儿童的母亲免疫知识水平及其与充分免疫状态的关系。
设计与对象
一项基于社区的横断面研究对 2023 年至 2024 年间在尼日利亚河流州 Asari-Toru 地方政府区 (ASALGA) 通过多阶段抽样选出的 374 名母亲或主要照顾者进行了研究。
核心方法
知识采用包含 13 个项目的访谈员管理量表(分数 0-13)进行测量,并分为良好(9-13)、一般(5-8)或差(0-4)。使用卡方检验和二元逻辑回归检查与完全免疫的关联。
主要结果
只有25.7%的母亲知识良好,48.7%的母亲知识一般,25.7%的母亲知识较差。知识良好、一般和较差的母亲的孩子的完全免疫覆盖率分别为 52.1%、30.8% 和 16.7% (p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
良好的知识仍然与完全免疫独立相关(AOR = 5.22;95% CI:2.46–11.08)。该研究强调需要进行有针对性的健康教育以提高 疫苗接种 率。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,提高母亲对特定 疫苗接种 时间表的了解可以提高河流社区的免疫覆盖率。这对旨在提高 疫苗接种 比率的公共卫生策略具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。由于依赖自我报告数据且缺乏纵向随访,证据强度中等。

Maternal Knowledge of Childhood Immunization and Its Association with Full Immunization Status Among Mothers of Children Aged 12–23 Months in a Riverine Community of Rivers State, Nigeria

Authors: Ibinabo Cotterell
Original abstract (English)
Maternal knowledge is a modifiable determinant of childhood vaccination, yet little is known about its level among mothers in the riverine communities of the Niger Delta. This study assessed the level of maternal knowledge about immunisation and its association with full immunisation status among children aged 12–23 months in Asari-Toru Local Government Area (ASALGA), Rivers State, Nigeria. A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 374 mothers or primary caregivers selected through multistage sampling. Knowledge was measured with a 13-item interviewer-administered scale (score 0–13) and categorised as good (9–13), fair (5–8) or poor (0–4). Associations with full immunisation were examined using chi-square tests and binary logistic regression. Only 25.7% of mothers had good knowledge, 48.7% had fair knowledge and 25.7% had poor knowledge. Although 93.0% had heard of childhood immunisation, only 49.2% knew the age for measles vaccination. Full immunisation coverage was 52.1%, 30.8% and 16.7% among children of mothers with good, fair and poor knowledge, respectively (p < 0.001). Good knowledge remained independently associated with full immunisation (AOR = 5.22; 95% CI: 2.46–11.08). Awareness of immunisation is high, but specific, actionable knowledge of the schedule is deficient and is associated with incomplete vaccination. Schedule-specific, culturally adapted health education delivered beyond clinic walls should be prioritised in riverine communities.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Maternal knowledge is a modifiable determinant of childhood vaccination, yet little is known about its level among mothers in the riverine communities of the Niger Delta. This study aimed to assess the level of maternal knowledge about immunisation and its association with full immunisation status among children aged 12–23 months.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional study was conducted among 374 mothers or primary caregivers selected through multistage sampling in Asari-Toru Local Government Area (ASALGA), Rivers State, Nigeria, between 2023 and 2024.
Core methods
Knowledge was measured with a 13-item interviewer-administered scale (score 0–13) and categorised as good (9–13), fair (5–8) or poor (0–4). Associations with full immunisation were examined using chi-square tests and binary logistic regression.
Main results
Only 25.7% of mothers had good knowledge, 48.7% had fair knowledge and 25.7% had poor knowledge. Full immunisation coverage was 52.1%, 30.8% and 16.7% among children of mothers with good, fair and poor knowledge, respectively (p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
Good knowledge remained independently associated with full immunisation (AOR = 5.22; 95% CI: 2.46–11.08). The study highlights the need for targeted health education to improve vaccination rates.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that improving maternal knowledge of specific vaccination schedules could enhance immunisation coverage in riverine communities. This has implications for public health strategies aimed at increasing vaccination rates.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on self-reported data and the lack of longitudinal follow-up.

使用 Swin-transformer 和特征选择技术的猴痘疾病 诊断 的深层框架

A deep framework for Monkeypox disease diagnosis using Swin-transformer and feature selection techniques
作者： Jihad Anwar Qadir, Jihad A. Qadir
摘要中文翻译本研究介绍了一种用于多类皮肤疾病诊断的新方法。应用了特征选择(FS)方法,包括主成分分析(PCA)、互信息、卡方(\(\chi ^2\))和递归特征消除(RFE),以识别最相关的特征,减少噪声和计算开销。所提出的方法在原始图像上使用卡方(\(\chi ^2\))和RFE与随机森林结合,实现了98.31%的准确率、98.31%的召回率、98.36%的精确率和98.33%的F1分数。这些结果表明,结合有效的特征提取和选择技术与强大的分类器,可以为自动化皮肤疾病诊断提供高准确率和高效率的解决方案。类似内容正在被其他人查看数据可用性:支持本研究发现的数据集可在Mendeley Data仓库中公开获取,并可通过https://doi.org/10.17632/r9bfpnvyxr.6进行访问。p 2024 - Ali SN,Ahmed MT,Paul J,Jahan T,Sani SM,Noor N,Hasan T (2022) 使用深度学习模型检测猴痘皮肤病变:一项可行性研究。arXiv:2207.03342 arXiv预印本 - Saha e.a. S (2023) 对于猴痘检测的CNN预训练模型比较研究。摘要:猴痘、水痘、麻疹以及其他皮肤病由于其视觉相似性和病变表现的变异性,对准确和及时的诊断构成了重大挑战。借助人工智能的计算机辅助诊断(CAD)系统已出现作为强大工具,以协助早期和准确地检测猴痘等疾病,从而改善医疗保健结果。该方法利用Swin Transformer(ST)作为特征提取器,利用其在高维图像中捕捉复杂视觉模式的能力...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决准确诊断猴痘和其他视觉上相似的皮肤病的挑战,由于它们的临床相似性和病变表现的多样性,这些疾病一直是一个重大的公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
该研究采用了自动皮肤病 诊断 的深度学习框架,重点关注使用皮肤图像数据集的猴痘。该方法涉及特征提取和选择技术以提高诊断准确性。
核心方法
该研究使用 Swin Transformer 进行特征提取,并应用主成分分析 (PCA)、互信息、卡方 (\(\chi^2\)) 和递归特征消除 (RFE) 等特征选择方法来识别相关特征。然后使用机器学习分类器对这些优化后的特征进行分类,包括随机森林 (RF)、支持 媒介 机器 (SVM)、极限梯度提升 (XGBoost) 和朴素贝叶斯 (NB)。
主要结果
该方法使用 \(\chi^2\) 和带有随机森林的 RFE 在原始图像上实现了 98.31% 的准确率、98.31% 的召回率、98.36% 的精度和 98.33% 的 F1 分数。与之前的模型相比,准确率提高了 4.44%。
解释与创新
作者将这些结果解释为证明了将特征提取和选择技术与稳健的分类器相结合以实现自动化 Monkeypox 诊断 的有效性。新颖之处在于 Swin Transformer 与特征选择方法的集成,以提高诊断性能。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为自动化猴痘 诊断 提供了高度准确和高效的解决方案,有助于早期检测并改善医疗保健结果,特别是在资源有限的环境中,快速可靠的诊断工具至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于皮肤图像数据集,虽然该方法很稳健,但对现实临床环境的普遍适用性可能有限。由于依赖单一数据集并且缺乏直接的临床验证,证据是中等的。

A deep framework for Monkeypox disease diagnosis using Swin-transformer and feature selection techniques

Authors: Jihad Anwar Qadir, Jihad A. Qadir
Original abstract (English)
This study introduces a novel approach for multi-class skin disease diagnosis. Feature selection (FS) methods, including Principal Component Analysis (PCA), Mutual Information, Chi-square (\(\chi ^2\)), and Recursive Feature Elimination (RFE), are applied to identify the most relevant features, reducing noise and computational overhead. The proposed method achieves 98.31% accuracy, 98.31% recall, 98.36% precision, and 98.33% F1-score using \(\chi ^2\) and RFE with Random Forest on original images. These results demonstrate that combining effective feature extraction and selection techniques with robust classifiers provides a highly accurate and efficient solution for automated skin disease diagnosis. The methodology utilizes the Swin Transformer (ST) as a feature extractor, leveraging its ability to capture complex visual patterns in high-dimensional image...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenges of accurately diagnosing Monkeypox and other visually similar skin diseases, which have been a significant public health concern due to their clinical resemblance and variability in lesion presentation.
Design and population
The study employed a deep learning framework for automated skin disease diagnosis, focusing on Monkeypox using a dataset of skin images. The methodology involved feature extraction and selection techniques to enhance diagnostic accuracy.
Core methods
The study used the Swin Transformer for feature extraction and applied feature selection methods such as Principal Component Analysis (PCA), Mutual Information, Chi-square (\(\chi^2\)), and Recursive Feature Elimination (RFE) to identify relevant features. These optimized features were then classified using machine learning classifiers including Random Forest (RF), Support Vector Machine (SVM), Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), and Naive Bayes (NB).
Main results
The proposed method achieved 98.31% accuracy, 98.31% recall, 98.36% precision, and 98.33% F1-score using \(\chi^2\) and RFE with Random Forest on original images. This represents a 4.44% improvement in accuracy compared to previous models.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these results as demonstrating the effectiveness of combining feature extraction and selection techniques with robust classifiers for automated Monkeypox diagnosis. The novelty lies in the integration of Swin Transformer with feature selection methods for improved diagnostic performance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a highly accurate and efficient solution for automated Monkeypox diagnosis, which can aid in early detection and improve healthcare outcomes, particularly in resource-limited settings where rapid and reliable diagnostic tools are essential.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on a dataset of skin images, and while the methodology is robust, the generalizability to real-world clinical settings may be limited. The evidence is moderate due to the reliance on a single dataset and the absence of direct clinical validation.

随着另一种病毒成为人们关注的焦点,麻疹 悄然回归。

Original abstract (English)
Measles is reemerging, with cases and outbreaks increasing across the United States and other countries where measles had previously been eliminated. physicians have no experience with treating measles. However, the increasing likelihood of encountering not only children but also adults with measles requires a clinical review of the epidemiology, prevention, diagnosis, and management of this previously uncommon infection for general internal medicine physicians and hospitalists who care for adults in settings with measles reemergence. Prompt clinical suspicion, infection control measures, and notification of public health departments support timely diagnosis and disease control.

麻疹 麻疹 覆盖范围的下降和 麻疹 在美洲的复兴:对 发病率 和 暴发 关联的系统审查

Declining Measles Vaccination Coverage and Measles Resurgence in the Americas: A Systematic Review of Associations with Incidence and Outbreaks

Authors: Paula Nevack de Britto Goulart
Original abstract (English)
This systematic review examines the association between insufficient or declining population-level measles vaccination coverage and measles incidence or outbreak occurrence in the Americas. The review focuses on observational epidemiological evidence, including ecological, longitudinal, and spatial analyses using observed vaccination coverage and measles outcomes. Searches were conducted in PubMed/MEDLINE, LILACS, and SciELO, covering publications from November 2, 2016, to November 2, 2026. The review includes evidence from national and subnational populations and considers statistically significant and non-significant associations. Bibliographic screening has been conducted, and the eligibility and quantitative findings of candidate studies are being verified. Final risk-of-bias assessment and evidence synthesis remain in progress.

多发性硬化症和病毒:《危险关系》。

Multiple Sclerosis and Viruses: « Liaisons Dangereuses ».
作者： Samanta Plavina, Daniela Malakovska, Jolanta Kalnina, Ilva Trapina, Elina Leonova, Mohammed Habib, Susanne Schifmann, Jegors Paramonovs, Nikolajs Sjakste, Yegor Vassetzky
摘要中文翻译多发性硬化症(MS)是一种复杂的、多因素的神经退行性疾病,其病因仍部分不明。与MS发病相关的风险因素包括遗传易感性和多种环境应激因素;然而,最近病毒感染引起了广泛关注,被认为是重要的环境诱因。尽管已知EB病毒(EBV)与MS易感性之间存在明确的关联,但单一病原体模型无法清晰描述驱动该疾病分子级联反应的机制,这表明从感染到自身免疫反应的转变可能涉及不止一种病毒。在本综述中,我们探讨了不同神经嗜性病毒之间的相互作用可能在MS中发挥协同致病作用的假设,这一领域目前在文献中研究较少。我们总结了不同病毒在MS发病中作用的矛盾证据,包括疱疹病毒科(人类巨细胞病毒、EB病毒、单纯疱疹病毒HSV-1和HSV-2,以及水痘-带状疱疹病毒)、副粘病毒科(麻疹病毒病毒和风疹病毒)、流感病毒、SARS-CoV-2以及人类内源性逆转录病毒(HERVs)。这些病毒可能通过在共享分子通路中的相互作用、免疫调节以及激活潜伏感染,共同影响MS中特征性的炎症和脱髓鞘过程。本综述评估了病毒相互作用在MS病因中的作用,通过这些病原体的累积和交互效应。现有的流行病学、免疫学和实验数据表明,病毒的共同暴露可能在遗传易感个体中协同影响MS的易感性。明确这些病毒相互作用为识别新型生物标志物和通过靶向控制病毒网络开发预防策略提供了新的研究方向。

Multiple Sclerosis and Viruses: « Liaisons Dangereuses ».

Authors: Samanta Plavina, Daniela Malakovska, Jolanta Kalnina, Ilva Trapina, Elina Leonova, Mohammed Habib, Susanne Schifmann, Jegors Paramonovs, Nikolajs Sjakste, Yegor Vassetzky
Original abstract (English)
Multiple sclerosis (MS) is a complex, multifactorial neurodegenerative disease with an aetiology that is still partially elusive. Several risk factors are associated with MS development including genetic predisposition and various environmental stressors; however, recently, viral infections have gained significant attention as a crucial environmental trigger. Despite the well-established link between Epstein-Barr virus (EBV) and MS susceptibility, a single-pathogen model fails to clearly characterise the molecular cascades driving the disease, suggesting that the transition from infection to autoimmune response involves more than one viral agent. In this review we examine the speculation that the interactions among different neurotropic viruses could serve a collective causative role in MS, which is under-researched in the literature at present. We summarise contradictory evidence on the role of different viral agents, including the Herpesviridae family (Human Cytomegalovirus, EBV, Herpes Simplex Viruses HSV-1 and HSV-2, and Varicella Zoster Virus), Paramyxoviridae family (Measles Virus and Rubella Virus), Influenza virus, SARS-CoV-2, and Human Endogenous Retroviruses (HERVs). Through interplay within shared molecular pathways, immune modulation, and activation of latent infections, these viruses may collectively influence the inflammatory and demyelinating processes that characterise MS. This review evaluates the etiological role of viral interplay in MS through the cumulative and interactive effects of these pathogens. The available epidemiological, immunological, and experimental data converge on the concept that viral co-exposures act synergistically to shape MS susceptibility in genetically predisposed individuals. Characterising these viral interactions presents a new research direction for the identification of novel biomarkers and the development of preventative strategies through the targeted control of viral networks.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
In this review we examine the speculation that the interactions among different neurotropic viruses could serve a collective causative role in MS, which is under-researched in the literature at present.
Evidence base and method

Controversies and gaps

Research and practice implications
